# Project Memory

This document is the current-state memory cache for Royal Regent Nexus. It is not a development log.

- Keep only long-lived facts that are still true and useful to future agents.
- When a fact changes, replace it in place and remove the obsolete statement.
- Do not append task reports, verification history, Git activity, local-machine incidents, or resolved work.
- Current source code, registered routes, database schema and tests take precedence over this document.
- Read this file selectively by task keywords unless the task requires a broad architectural review.

## 1. Product Scope

Royal Regent Nexus is a multi-factory manufacturing operations portal for group-level and factory-level work. The current factory catalog contains:

- Group scope: `group`
- Factories: `huakang-a`, `huakang-b`, `huakang-c`, `huakang-d`, `huadeng`, `huaxing`

The application combines implemented operational domains, file-processing tools, and module-discovery cards whose maturity varies. A visible module card does not by itself prove that a persistent backend domain exists.

The main implemented or partially implemented domains are:

- Account login, sessions, users, roles, permissions and factory isolation
- Molding-sample production tasks, material requisitions and factory inventory
- Global raw-material master data
- Internal quote collaboration and controlled customer-price conversion
- Customer pricing records
- Huaxing BuzzBee, Dickie, Caixing, EDU, 360, Yinhui, SEASONS, Maxx, Shushupapa, Barter and Disney; Huadeng Casdon, Jakks, Simba, Spin and Spin Master; Huakang A 360, Green Toys and HeadStart; plus Huakang C INDEX, JAZAWARES/JAZWARES, MAXX, STROTTMAN and JP customer-order preview and schedule export
- Carton-mark comparison
- Indonesia invoice reconciliation

The Customer Order Center scope is order intake, sending confirmed orders to downstream modules, and subsequent shipment confirmation. It owns original purchase orders, normalized order facts, validation, confirmation, immutable versions, source lineage, dispatch records and confirmed shipped/remaining quantities. The main order-processing flow ends at dispatch to PMC, warehouses and the injection department. Detailed material requirements, purchasing needs, injection decisions, workshop scheduling and inventory records are calculated and maintained downstream, without requiring the order center to maintain or aggregate those details. Physical shipping execution remains downstream. The agreed first implementation uses authorized Sales personnel to confirm actual shipment date, quantity and shipping document number, with append-only reversal evidence for corrections; automatic warehouse feedback remains future work.

The agreed direction for maintaining customer schedules, factory-wide and unshipped/shipped-order views, order dispatch and shipment confirmation is recorded in `docs/business/customer-order-center-erp-roadmap.md`; sections 13–15 separate the implemented ledger, historical migration and customer schedule views from remaining business cutover work. Material/BOM expansion and detailed injection planning are outside this order-center scope.

### Kingdee Replacement Scope and Reference Workflows

The user intends to build a system replacing the existing Kingdee ERP. The reference manuals are `销售订单&预测单&放产订单操作手册(1).docx`, `采购申请&采购订单、采购取价操作手册(1).docx`, `生产订单操作手册(1).docx`, `委外流程-操作手册(1).docx` and `生产&委外领料&生产&委外&采购入库V1(1).docx`. Their business screenshots principally show 金蝶云·星空 标准版, with mixed historical dates, organizations and enterprise-demo screenshots. They establish reference operating procedures, not the exact current deployment/version or universally accepted replacement rules. The final factory scope, financial/accounting scope and cutover boundary remain unconfirmed.

The documented demand chain is sales orders or advance-stock forecasts → 放产单 → purchase requisitions, production orders or subcontract orders. 放产单 carries source-document references, plan tracking, loss inputs and material-demand rows; the illustrated calculation mode is `只计算需求用量`. Inventory/on-order netting, forecast consumption, loss formulas, BOM expansion/version rules and whether this document is customized must be verified from the actual configuration and worked examples. Do not treat a column named 净需求 as proof of full MRP, conflate plan tracking/product codes with stable document-line identities, or move downstream material planning into the Customer Order Center without an explicit scope change.

The manuals distinguish document approval from production/subcontract line execution. Production picking requires release to 下达 and references the production material list; production receipt references the production order, with a documented instruction to finish picking first. Subcontracting requires appropriate supplier/material capabilities, an approved material list and released subcontract order, then a subcontract purchase order before picking; receipt references that purchase order. Manual subcontract-order creation without a 放产单 is documented, and subcontract closure also requires closing its purchase order. Inventory documents update stock only after approval in the described procedure. Purchase orders have one supplier; material defaults can suggest that supplier. Pricing uses approved effective-dated price lists/adjustments or the previous purchase price via 取参考价 before approval. Exact price-selection precedence, partial/over-issue and receipt rules, reversals, subcontract material reconciliation and approval ownership still require validation. Conflicting reverse-execution wording/screenshots must not become an implementation contract by guesswork.

## 2. Active Technical Baseline

- Codex uses client/user configuration and built-in defaults without repository-specific model, reasoning-effort, concurrency or custom subagent overrides. The previous six-role routing configuration and mandatory role assignments are removed. General development, verification and Git rules remain in `AGENTS.md`; `docs/agent-routing/context-index.md` is only a project-fact lookup index. User-level credentials, providers, MCP, plugins and permissions are managed outside this repository.
- Frontend: Vue 3, TypeScript, Vite, Pinia, Vue Router, Axios, Tailwind CSS and shadcn-vue/reka components.
- Backend: FastAPI, SQLAlchemy, Alembic and server-side session authentication.
- Production database: PostgreSQL. Local development may use SQLite where the current configuration permits it.
- Production packaging: separate API, Web and document-tools worker containers with PostgreSQL and Nginx. Compose commands must include `.env.production`, `docker-compose.prod.yml` and server-local `.deployment-prod-release.yml`. The current pinned production revision, image identities, verification limits and recovery paths are maintained in section 7. Future releases must retain the bounded order-file process boundary, sparse insertion and single API worker required by the carton task registry.
- The Web image normalizes static directories/files to 755/644 because Vite preserves public-file permissions from the checkout. Nginx explicitly serves `.mjs` workers as JavaScript. Browser acceptance must cover the HTTP origin, Logo decoding and PDF canvas rendering, beyond entry-asset hashes and API health. The current PDF.js 6.3.289 runtime requires modern browser APIs including `Uint8Array.prototype.toHex`; older browsers missing it cannot render PDFs. Public-origin Logo/PDF acceptance passes in Chrome 154, while Edge 135 has this compatibility limitation.
- Business timestamps are interpreted and displayed in `Asia/Shanghai`.
- API routing is rooted under `/api`; application health is exposed through `/health`.
- The former AI assistant, Workbench, Provider integration, cloud document enhancement, background Tasks, Artifacts, Vision, Skills, Prompts, controlled actions, operational alerts and scheduling layout inference have been removed. No `/api/ai/*` or `/workbench/ai` route remains. Historical Alembic revisions are retained only to preserve upgrade-chain continuity; migration `20260826_0084` removes their runtime tables and alert rows.
- 曜灵 · Nexus AI is a new, independent assistant under `/api/assistant`, `src/features/assistant` and `src/stores/assistant.ts`, hosted from `App.vue` across authenticated normal/fullPage routes. It does not restore the retired subsystem. Ordinary auth refresh preserves runs; user/epoch changes clear private state. Server history, attachments and runs enforce the current user plus employment epoch; suppliers receive only supplier/general help. Migration `20260929_0131` adds four `nexus_assistant_*` tables after `0130`, explicitly excluded from startup `create_all`. Code defaults remain disabled. The D:/RR/royal-regent-nexus local override enables the UI and deterministic help, with its own SQLite at 20261008_0140; this merge retains assistant revision 20260929_0131 and published main revision 20261006_0139. The local override has an independent Beijing Qwen API key restricted to qwen3.7-plus, a workspace-specific compatible-mode/v1 endpoint and an ignored local capability profile. Real synthetic Qwen probes have verified text, auto/on/off thinking, vision, tool round-trip and multi-turn context; the matching local capability profile enables these combinations. After account arrears caused 403 responses, the user recharged and real browser end-to-end acceptance passed with the original credentials: multi-turn history, image upload/removal/reselection, reasoning plus help-tool round-trip, cancellation and history after reload. These checks use an isolated synthetic database and account. The file input resets after selection so the same image can be selected again after removal or upload failure. Capability verification timestamps are historical evidence, not live provider health. Provider byte/frame iterators are explicitly closed before their HTTP contexts exit. The assistant uses explicitly pinned httpx 0.28.1; the Linux Python 3.13 release probe exposed an httpx2/httpcore2 generator-shutdown error after a completed real stream. Other modules retain their existing HTTP clients. Secrets stay in backend/.env, outside Git and frontend bundles; see docs/assistant/MODEL_CONNECTION.md. Other checkout databases and production are separate. SSE uses shared database admission, leases and cancellation, explicit recovery, and no automatic paid retry. `shared/assistant-help` is server-only and source-fingerprinted; 16 verified articles cover 15 module overviews and one injection field; the former nine partial overviews now include source-checked fields, states, steps and permission limits. This is bounded overview coverage, not every field or child page. No live business query/write tools or web-search adapter exist. Optional images/thinking/tools remain hidden until a matching verified capability profile enables them. Assistant files use their own `assistant-assets` volume. Real NVDA phase announcements, actual 200% Chromium page zoom and Chromium 24px fonts have been checked with synthetic data. Desktop assistants default to a slim edge tab: mouse hover opens after 180ms without taking focus, and leaving both tab and panel collapses after 550ms unless the panel retains keyboard/editor focus or is in focus mode. Touch/narrow screens use click, and Alt+J remains available. Reopening anchors beside the edge tab so the hover path stays reachable. Expanded panels move freely from the non-action title bar, default to 432x640, and support a corner width/height resize within 360-600 by 360-820 and viewport limits. Size and edge preferences survive collapse, navigation and reload; smaller viewports clamp the visible panel. Arrow keys adjust movement/size, Shift increases the step, and Home or the reset button restores defaults. Narrow screens and focus mode hide free-drag controls; business modals still take priority. Physical phone keyboard/safe-area/rotation, OS text scaling and a full clean Docker/Linux dependency install remain unverified; a fresh virtual environment only proves assistant dependency and HTTP/SSE lifecycle compatibility. Frontend full regression passed before the latest main merge; the old backend full run stopped at 25 percent when sources changed, retaining legacy-module failures. Release migration 20261008_0147 joins the existing assistant merge 20261008_0140 with the published 20261008_0146 branch without changing either applied history. Local browser QA and isolated SQLite/PostgreSQL migrations do not establish production readiness. Current activation, rollback, validation evidence and limits are in `docs/assistant/IMPLEMENTATION.md` and `RUNBOOK.md`; the independent `/image-translation/` app has no assistant host.

## 3. Architecture and Source-of-Truth Entry Points

Use these entry points before relying on documentation or memory:

- Image/PDF translation: the independently built React workspace under `shinobu-web/` is opened from the Vue shared tool center at `/image-translation/`; it originated as a source snapshot and does not sync with the original project. Entry and deployment details are in `shinobu-web/README_FOR_RR.md` and `docs/document-tools/image-translation.md`.
- Frontend bootstrap and navigation: `src/main.ts`, `src/router/index.ts`
- Page-entry policy: `src/config/pageAccessPolicy.ts`
- Module and factory discovery metadata: `src/data/enterpriseMock.ts`
- Frontend API clients: `src/api/`
- Frontend domain state: `src/stores/`
- FastAPI application and registered routers: `backend/app/main.py`
- Database initialization and schema guards: `backend/app/db.py`
- Backend routes: `backend/app/api/`
- Persistence models: `backend/app/models/`
- Business services: `backend/app/services/`
- Canonical permission codes: `backend/app/services/permission_codes.py`
- Migrations: `backend/alembic/versions/`
- Deployment workflow: `deploy/update-from-github.sh`
- Runtime composition: `docker-compose.prod.yml` and the production Dockerfiles
- Current behavior contracts: tests adjacent to the relevant frontend or backend domain
- Frontend design-system guidance: `DESIGN.md`; executable global tokens and shared shell styles remain in `src/style.css`, with shared component contracts under `src/components/ui/` and `src/components/common/` and explicitly scoped feature exceptions documented in the design guide.

`src/data/enterpriseMock.ts` is a navigation/catalog and demonstration-data source. It is not authoritative business data and must not be treated as evidence that the corresponding backend, permissions or persistence layer exists.

For internal-quote business context, consult `docs/business/internal-quote-collaboration-design.md`, then verify all claims against current models, services, routes, migrations and tests.

## 4. Global Business Invariants

- Every factory-scoped request and write must use a validated, explicit factory identifier.
- Data must not silently fall back to another factory or aggregate across factories.
- Cross-factory access exists only where current backend policy explicitly grants it.
- The global raw-material master is the deliberate exception to factory-scoped master data; inventory and movements remain factory-scoped.
- Backend authorization and data filtering are authoritative. Frontend visibility or disabled controls are not security boundaries.
- Identifiers such as purchase-order numbers, item numbers, material codes and document numbers are strings; do not coerce them to numbers or remove leading zeros.
- Uploaded source workbooks and PDFs are inputs. Processing must create a new output artifact rather than mutate the source file.
- Server-side calculations and persisted structured data are authoritative for commercial totals; client-submitted totals must be recalculated or validated.
- Customer-facing quote conversion must use an explicit whitelist of approved fields. Internal-only fields must never be copied merely because they are present in a source object or workbook.
- Published or approved artifacts must retain their source lineage and revision identity.
- Secrets, passwords, access tokens and machine-specific paths do not belong in project memory.
- `/api/injection` belongs to the molding-sample production domain. It is not the injection-scheduling-center backend.

## 5. Authentication, Permissions and Factory Isolation

- Authentication uses account credentials and a server-side `AuthSession`.
- The browser session cookie is `rr_session`, HttpOnly and SameSite `lax`; production configuration is expected to make it Secure.
- Registration and password-reset requests are independent persisted account workflows. Password reset uses an original-browser claim: submission stores only a SHA-256 claim hash while the raw random claim exists only in a 48-hour HttpOnly Cookie. Permission- and scope-controlled approval additionally requires explicit identity verification and only opens a four-hour completion window; it does not change the password, set forced-change state, revoke sessions or return a secret. Successful claim completion atomically changes the password, revokes every active session, completes the request and invalidates the user's other active reset requests. Claim-less legacy pending/approved requests cannot fall back to temporary-password issuance and require a fresh submission.
- The former PIN workflow and PIN endpoints are not part of the current product. Operational workflows use authenticated accounts, roles and permissions.
- System positions are fixed, code-owned templates and are reconciled from the backend catalog. Custom roles are database-managed. V2 assignment bindings pin immutable role-definition versions; later template reconciliation cannot silently grow their permissions. A deliberate identity package upgrade is required.
- Effective access is derived from account state, active assignment sources, employment epoch, pinned role versions, explicit factory ceilings, exact department scope, direct overrides and explicit denies. Legacy users retain their existing source interpretation. V2 users always use canonical checks, even if the general rollout mode changes; expired, revoked or mismatched sources cannot fall back to live roles. Runtime UTC half-open intervals drive identity and authorization without requiring a worker.
- Explicit denies remain authoritative.
- The general-manager position receives business permissions and cross-factory operating scope, but does not implicitly receive `system:*` administration permissions.
- The warehouse manager (`position_warehouse_manager`) operates the 22 PMC warehouse permissions across factories, including fabric purchase-source read/import/receive and carton-mark original-file read/upload; warehouse supervisors and keepers retain own-factory scope. All three still share the existing cross-factory production-task read-only entitlement. The manager's shared order-inbox permissions stay in PMC/warehouse rather than injection, and department-owned notifications and fabric permissions remain department-sensitive; separate valid position grants remain independent. The fabric API evaluates canonical factory/department permissions and explicit denies in every rollout mode. This does not add engineering/production workflow approvals, carton customer management/order adjustment or IAM administration, nor expand pinned V2 role versions.
- Wildcard system-administrator access is a separate authority.
- Authorization supports `legacy`, `shadow` and `enforce` modes. General IAM configuration writes are valid only in `enforce` mode, and active general configurable overrides require that mode to remain enforced. The three `carton_supplier:*` global user overrides are an explicit narrow exception: a group superadministrator may preview and commit only those overrides while other modules remain in legacy mode; supplier endpoints evaluate them canonically without changing other modules' rollout mode.
- The current frontend page-entry policy allows authenticated users to enter pages in read-only mode even without the page permission. This affects navigation only; backend reads, writes and sensitive data remain permission- and scope-controlled.
- Permission codes are defined centrally in `backend/app/services/permission_codes.py`; do not invent frontend-only permission identifiers.
- Factory isolation must be enforced in backend queries and mutations, not inferred from the selected factory in the UI.
- The frontend initializes its in-memory factory context from the authenticated employee's `profile.primary_factory_id` before the initial shell/protected route renders. A validated destination `query.factory` (including a safe login/password-change redirect) takes priority. New login, restored session or changed account initializes once; same-account, same-profile refreshes and avatar updates preserve the current manual choice. An official primary-factory change normally refreshes the default, but an open /modules/ business route pins its current factory until the user leaves it, so unfinished forms cannot silently submit into the new factory. The identity refresh banner asks the user to recheck scope before submitting. Logout/session expiry clears the binding. Wildcard, group, missing or invalid primary factories resolve to neutral `group`, never to another factory inferred from roles or permission scopes. No factory selection or binding is persisted in browser storage. The existing `activeProductionFactory` group fallback is a separate production-component contract and remains unchanged.

## 6. Active Modules

### Accounts and IAM

The backend exposes account login/session behavior, registration and same-browser password-reset claim workflows, forced password change for independently flagged accounts, user management, role management, fixed system positions, effective-access calculation and permission administration. Password-reset requests have their own persisted state and notification linkage; notifications are navigation signals rather than the workflow source of truth. The public claim status and completion endpoints reveal no employee profile or review detail, and losing the original-browser Cookie requires a new application. IAM changes follow the configured authorization rollout mode, apart from the narrowly scoped supplier-permission exception above. Default grants and code-owned system positions are reconciled from code rather than edited as arbitrary database records. Registration and approval share a department catalog that includes three-d-printing, matching the registration form and the three built-in 3D positions. Registration remains pending until normal administrator approval; accepting the department does not grant printer control or broader factory access.

IAM V2 configuration defaults remain `IAM_IDENTITY_WRITES_ENABLED=false` and `IAM_IDENTITY_SCHEDULING_ENABLED=false`. The current local backend uses `AUTHZ_MODE=legacy`, `AUTHZ_WRITES_ENABLED=false`, `IAM_IDENTITY_WRITES_ENABLED=false` and `IAM_IDENTITY_SCHEDULING_ENABLED=false`; migration repair does not enable IAM writes or scheduling. The documented verified production deployment enables identity writes with `AUTHZ_MODE=enforce` and `AUTHZ_WRITES_ENABLED=true`. Production also enables scheduled changes; the local scheduling flag remains disabled. Scheduling supports future transfers, added assignments and assignment endings after explicit formal-identity confirmation. Authorization resolves the approved timeline at the effective instant independently of the API background worker; the worker updates profile projections, handover state and notifications every 30 seconds. Disabling scheduling prevents new bookings but does not cancel approved future changes. Production has completed migration `20260926_0125`, restored-copy rehearsal and a read-only comparison of all 163 existing users at one fixed time, with no permission, source, account or session changes. Enabling the local entrypoint does not enroll existing users or change their authority sources; legacy users still need explicit confirmation. Existing account databases require additive migration `20260926_0125` before starting this API, even with identity writes disabled. Formal history uses `EmployeeAssignment`, immutable `IamRoleVersion`, source metadata and employment epochs; `EmployeeProfile` remains a compatibility projection. The six production factories and functional `group-management` organization are distinct; group staff have no implicit production-factory grant. Legacy confirmation requires explicit source classification and permission-preserving preview. Shared database mutation locking, fresh actors, dependency fingerprints, optimistic revisions and idempotency receipts protect new and existing IAM writers. V2 users cannot use the old system-position cleanup; freeze revokes sessions, leave ends current/future allow sources and advances the epoch, and rehire is explicit. The new personnel center at `/system/users` retains registration/password recovery at `/system/users/registration`; requests and audit have real pages. Role/system administration and supplier sources remain independently classified.

The IAM frontend uses a persistent `/system` workspace around the six existing IAM routes, with page-group transitions, measured permission-aware navigation and shared Reka dialogs/disclosures. People query links (`?person=`), registration/password-recovery query links and the `VITE_IAM_IDENTITY_UI_ENABLED=false` compatibility path remain supported. Personnel changes use a three-step wizard; a saved draft survives preview failures, changed fields invalidate previews, and unknown commit outcomes are checked by the existing single-change GET before retrying. List/detail/preview requests ignore obsolete responses; authorization denials clear previously displayed protected data. Personal authorization starts with individual exceptions and supplier collaboration; legacy position cleanup is hidden only when an authorized response has established V2 mode. The existing user-access GET returns the minimal `identity_mode` (`legacy` or `v2`) after its unchanged read guard, enabling direct-entry mode detection without requesting full personnel details. Access-manage-only viewers retain their existing legacy read scope and remain unable to read V2/full identity details without the required user-management scope. The frontend accepts older responses without this field as unknown unless the same viewer already has authoritative identity context; URL hints are never trusted. Switching the viewer or target clears displayed access and invalidates pending responses; identity context stays viewer-scoped and in memory. Audit filtering preserves the existing space-separated server wall-time contract because `created_at` is compared as text; its 100/200/500 result limits are not total-history counts. IAM styles are scoped under `rrn-iam-studio`/`iamx-*`; the old global identity stylesheet and repeated full-page IAM headers have been removed.


The identity handover adapter changes only editable internal quotes' current business owner with the existing resource lock, header revision and formal sales qualification. Historical creators and factories remain unchanged. Registration/password recovery remain role queues. Molding/production, warehouse/orders, carton/supplier, 3D, UV and spray responsibilities are explicitly uncovered and require manual checks. Notification outbox retries cannot alter effective authorization. Scheduled identity materialization is advisory; delayed handover reassessment is shown explicitly. Local acceptance covers worker-off time boundaries in auth, directories, old user lists, quote candidates and password recovery, frontend server-clock refresh, real PostgreSQL competition, migration interruption/recovery and an independently built V2-compatible fallback. An authorized independent agent review is complete. Production deployment and the immediate-change entrypoint are verified; no existing user was automatically enrolled. Administrators still explicitly confirm historical sources and actual personnel changes. Raw V1 binary rollback is unsafe. The fallback uses `backend/tools/build_iam_v2_compat.py`, a forced-disable backend entrypoint and `VITE_IAM_IDENTITY_UI_ENABLED=false`, while retaining canonical V2 authorization and existing runtime mode. Historical permission explanation uses current account state/current employment epoch, not a full historical account replay. Local evidence and rollout gates live in `docs/iam-v2-20260926/`.

Complete identity management scope includes current and future independent role/allow/deny sources as well as assignments; list filtering applies before totals and pagination. Old account-status and password-reset management paths use the same complete scope, shared mutation lock and freshly reloaded actor. Password-reset routing and visible profiles resolve the runtime primary identity rather than stale materialized profile fields. Registration approval classifies every role carrying system permissions as independent system administration, including custom roles.

The authenticated organization directory is a separate read-only contract under `/api/directory`; it does not reuse the permission-controlled `/api/system/users` administration payload. It exposes only active-account display fields from `AuthUser` and the runtime identity projection, with no login identifier, contact, role, permission, session or exact activity timestamp. `AuthUserPresence` stores only a coalesced server-time heartbeat: at most one write per user per 45 seconds, with online at 120 seconds or less, away through 15 minutes and offline thereafter. Frontend heartbeats and directory polling run only for an authenticated, online, visible page; presence heartbeats also pause during forced password change and resume after it completes. The module-center summary and open drawer use low-frequency polling while `/people` provides the complete paginated directory.

### Molding-Sample Production and Raw Materials

The molding-sample domain is the backend currently routed under `/api/injection`. It persists production orders and items, audit information, dispatch history, notifications, production problems, trial reports, material prices, requisitions, inventory batches and movements.

Engineering-origin factory and production factory are distinct concepts:

- Production is supported by Huakang A, Huakang B, Huadeng and Huaxing.
- Work originating from Huakang C or Huakang D must be dispatched explicitly to a supported production factory before production-side inventory or execution is applied.
- Requisitions, inventory and movements are scoped to the production factory.
- Production-task visibility and write capability are controlled independently by permission policy and explicit denies.

Raw-material master records are global and use `factory_id='*'`, with material code as the shared identity. Factory inventory is not global. Engineering and warehouse scopes may maintain the shared master only through the current permission rules.

### Internal Quote, Customer Price and Pricing

客价转换台提供“使用教程”弹窗，说明客户基础信息、报价接收与恢复预览、输出和 Dickie 合并流程；教程明确保存新参数后须恢复预览或重新导入，重复导出沿用当前参数快照。与客户订单中心共用 `UsageGuideDialog`，教程正文统一维护在 `src/data/salesUsageGuides.ts`。

Internal quotes are persistent and factory-scoped. Collaboration is organized into:

- Engineering
- Molding
- Assembly
- Painting
- Electronic
- Slush
- Sewing
- Hair
- Sales

Sales, engineering and assembly are mandatory in the current schema; the other sections are optional. Draft or rejected sections are editable, review states are reviewable, and approved or not-applicable sections are complete.

Internal-quote reviewers must belong to Sales (`sales-business`) and have effective review access in the quote's factory. A nonempty employee-profile department is authoritative; legacy accounts without it need an active, explicit Sales role binding. Engineering, management and wildcard administrator access alone never establish reviewer eligibility. Candidate lists, create/clone/header assignment and both section/whole review use this boundary; assignment names come from the active account rather than client input. Section self-review is controlled by `internal_quote:self_review`. Sales supervisors and sales managers receive it through their fixed positions, while administrators may grant or revoke it for an individual sales-business user through the existing personal-access override workflow. It bypasses submitter/reviewer separation only when the current user both created the quote and is its selected business owner; it never permits self-review on another creator's quote and does not change final release rules.

New internal quotes and ordinary clones default to `direct_output` (`module_version='v4'`). Existing `v2` section-review and `v3` whole-quote-review records retain their original evidence and workflow; explicit legacy API modes remain supported. Historical quotes enter the new workflow by copying a new version, without modifying the original. A v3 quote keeps department-owned editing and authoritative section calculations but rejects section submit/review/reopen actions. Once every participating section has saved content, a valid calculation and current dependencies, one authorized Sales submitter locks all participating sections in one immutable submission manifest. Only the selected business reviewer with effective Sales review access in the quote factory may approve or reject that whole submission. In v3, that reviewer may also be the submitter, regardless of who created the quote; this does not require the separate self-review permission. The existing self-review-only access fallback remains limited to the selected reviewer who created the quote, and v2 section self-review and legacy final-release separation remain unchanged. Approval marks every participating section approved and makes the quote eligible for controlled export; rejection marks every participating section rejected so the whole quote is unlocked while department edit permissions remain unchanged. The frontend creates and clones quotes into v4 and renders v3/v4 participating departments in one vertically continuous, always-full-width collaboration page. Quote Overview exposes one “添加、删除参与部门” panel to Sales/Engineering participation managers: every optional department is shown, checked means participating and unchecked means removed after an explicit data-impact confirmation; Sales, Engineering and Assembly remain fixed and cannot be removed. The left Page Navigation and right quote activity/cost sidebar are fixed edge drawers that stay collapsed without reserving layout width and expand on pointer hover or keyboard focus; the former manual focus-mode toggles and per-form block-dot navigators no longer exist. Page Navigation links Quote Overview, each department and Whole-Quote Actions, and department hover reveals live form-block completion. Whole-Quote Actions is the single bottom surface for summary/output and current-product save/switch/baseline copy; v4 exposes direct output, while v3 retains whole submission and authorized approve/reject. Its canonical process order is Engineering, Molding, Assembly, Painting, Electronic, Slush, Sewing, Hair, then Sales; API responses, create/clone participation, UI rendering, controlled export metadata and P4 artifact parsing follow the same sequence. Existing v2 quotes continue to render the legacy section-review interface.

The v4 alternative family belongs to one product and contains independent named scenarios and per-scenario versions (A-V2, B-V1, etc.). The collaboration page provides copy-as-scenario, copy-new-version, any-two comparison, customer adoption of an exact issued version, actual reported date, and per-version archive/restore with reasons. The two-version detail comparison groups saved business inputs by department and category, places item names, specifications, quantities and original-currency unit prices side by side, and highlights changed fields. Only differences are expanded by default; unchanged departments and rows can be opened on demand. It does not print internal IDs, raw calculation objects or English payload keys. New names appear as additions/deletions rather than guessing that renamed materials are identical; duplicate/reordered entries reserve exact matches before pairing same-name edits. The existing multi-quote amount comparison remains separate. Copying requires scoped read/create/clone access, source header revision and family revision; it carries the full current payload, all attachments with new IDs, molds and the exact reference snapshot into an independent single-product version record, then recalculates under the current formula. Logical document membership resolves through the family root and its original product batch: homepage lists/pagination/search show one original quotation, and opening any scenario/version retains all original product slots while selecting the current version for its own product. Physical version records stay separate so saves, totals and immutable exports never combine alternative costs; no schema migration or business-data rewrite is needed. Document metadata exposes product/version counts and authoritative deletion blockers. It never synchronizes sibling alternatives automatically or treats alternative costs as additive. Only explicit copying creates a version; ordinary saves update the same draft. Sales export permission allows direct issue once all participating sections have saved content, valid calculations and current dependencies. Issuing atomically seals sections, freezes cost context and summary, generates immutable `p4_direct_issued` workbook bytes and creates the customer-price handoff without fabricating approval records. Subsequent business changes require a copied version; download/retry returns the original bytes even after formula/layout changes. Every v4 content write, including imports and picture/supporting-attachment changes, advances the header revision so stale issue/copy requests are rejected. Selection/archive writes also enforce family revision and scoped Sales edit access; a selected version cannot be archived until deselected. Adoption is family-level metadata and can be managed while viewing an archived source, but its target must still be an unarchived issued version. Unused leaf scenario/version drafts may be deleted by their creator or a scoped Sales supervisor, including after archive. Submission/release/export/handoff evidence, actual reporting/adoption, or descendant references block deletion; original batch deletion also protects every product and its families. Quote/family locks guard cleanup, family revisions advance, audit evidence remains and frontend controls use the same backend blockers. Batch archive cannot bypass an issued member's frozen state. Customer conversion accepts both approved legacy P4 and direct-issued v4, keeps their existing validation/style contracts, and includes the source quote/version in individual customer export filenames. Migration `20260924_0119` adds the two family/alternative tables without rewriting historical quotes; existing databases must be explicitly migrated before startup. Explicit cross-scenario synchronization now previews and atomically applies selected cost-input categories within the same product family/customer/factory. It checks source/header/family revisions and a content-bound preview token under sorted quote locks followed by the family lock. Draft targets may update in place or copy a new version; issued targets require a new version, preserving original export bytes. Each changed department uses ordinary scoped edit checks, authoritative save/calculation and dependency invalidation; unchanged targets are skipped. Unselected categories, target reference prices/exchange rates, multipliers and customer supplements are retained. Retained rows reserve exact matches first; ambiguous duplicate rows with local supplements block rather than guessing ownership. Referenced source attachments are copied only as needed and equivalent target attachments are reused by department/content hash. Component-pricing quotations are currently blocked from bulk sync because component identities require an explicit mapping. The UI clears previews when selections/reason/source/family change and keeps completion results while refreshing the family list. Category sync replaces that category including deletions shown in preview; it is not a single-field patch or automatic background synchronization. Mixed-packaging production combinations remain outside this stage.

The internal-quote workspace exposes a shared, lazy-loaded usage tutorial from its home, collaboration, summary and export pages. It follows the carton tutorial interaction pattern with searchable chapters, workflow diagrams, local-only checklists and full-guide printing. Instructions distinguish current direct-output quotes from historical whole/section-review workflows; opening or closing the tutorial never navigates or saves quote content. Remaining scope and acceptance criteria are maintained in `docs/business/internal-quote-alternatives-followups.md`.

Internal-quote creation also supports product batches of at most 20 items. `single`, `series` and `multi_region` quote types share one batch quote number while every product remains a distinct internal quote with its own quantity, optional region code, main image, sections, calculation, summary and export. Each product may also receive Excel, Word, PDF or image source documents during creation; each document is assigned to a participating department and uploaded immediately after the product quote exists. Packaging-only copy is an explicit preview-and-confirm operation between different products in the same document/customer/factory, including a currently open scenario/version. It copies only Sales packaging materials, cartons, packaging dimensions and paper/JustPlay packaging inputs; optional Assembly packaging groups retain the target's assembly groups and labor base. Product/component names, quantity, all pictures, other departments, customer fields, multipliers and reference snapshots stay local. Costs are recalculated, all targets are revision-checked and locked before writing, stale previews reject and any failure rolls the entire operation back. The UI refuses unsaved source edits and distinguishes packaging reuse from whole-baseline overwrite. The first item is the baseline product; its current header, sections, frozen reference snapshot and non-image attachments may be copied once on demand into another still-mutable product, after which both products evolve independently. Product-level main images for ordinary customers and JustPlay are stored as `product-image` attachments and are not copied with the baseline. The collaboration header exposes a visible upload/replace button for editable quotes with scoped create access; uploads validate decoded image content and, when supplied, the header revision. Issued versions retain their original images and export bytes: users with create/clone access can choose a picture through “复制新版本并上传主图”, creating an independent draft version before upload. A failed upload preserves that draft and links to it for retry. JustPlay product main images are independent of its component images. Huakang B JustPlay instead collects one optional image for each main/accessory component at creation and allows replacement/deletion on the current-component collaboration panel. These use `component-image:<stable-component-id>` attachment departments, with quote/factory isolation, decoded-image validation, create permissions, header revision checks and draft/rejected-only writes. An image change increments the header revision and supersedes current exports. Adding a series product copies names/count but no images. A whole-baseline copy never imports source component images and retains target images only while both component ID and name still match; during baseline copying, legacy product pictures remain stored but are not guessed into JustPlay components. Product images and creation-time documents are previewable from the product overview and department material surfaces. Sales and engineering may read every quote attachment, while production users may read only product/component images and attachments assigned to their own production department; download and preview endpoints enforce the same boundary. List and dashboard endpoints expose only the batch root, while collaboration and summary pages provide product switching. The collaboration and output pages provide “系列报价一起输出”: users explicitly select one unarchived version per logical product slot and download one Excel with one visible product sheet per slot in batch order. The server checks membership, factory/customer, export permissions and unissued revisions under ordered quote/family locks; it atomically issues ready v4 drafts (or exports approved legacy products) and rolls the entire operation back if any product or workbook fails. Existing issued exports are reused without regeneration. Per-product supporting sheets remain veryHidden with isolated formula references, styles, images and print settings; source export IDs/hashes and the combined file hash are audited. Individual exports and customer-price handoffs remain separate authoritative records. Whole-quote submit, approve, reject and creator withdrawal operate atomically across every product in the batch, and archive or delete also covers the full batch. Before review completes, the creator of every product may use “退回修改” (`final-withdraw`) with a reason and current header revision, provided they retain factory-scoped create access. It changes pending v3 quotes and participating sections to editable rejected states, invalidates the submission, preserves content and submission evidence, handles the old review notifications, and records withdrawal audits without creating reviewer decisions. Re-submission is required; approved/exported quotes cannot be withdrawn. Whole submit, review and withdrawal acquire the same ordered product-row locks. Migration `20260817_0078` backfills every historical quote as a one-product batch without changing its existing workflow mode.

Historical quotation reuse during creation is separate from whole-baseline copying. Ordinary customers and Huakang B JustPlay can both choose exact historical products (including non-root batch products and regional variants), combine them with products from other quotations, and append manual products within the 20-product limit. JustPlay additionally accepts one optional source per destination component, allowing cross-product recombination or replacement of individual components after whole-product selection. `/internal-quotes/history-products` is paginated and factory-scoped, with quotation/product/customer search and customer/region filters; both catalogue and creation require factory-scoped read, create and clone access through Sales or Engineering. Creation verifies source fingerprints and component ownership, rejects ordinary/JustPlay mixing, copies data and attachments into independent draft quotes in one atomic batch transaction, remaps component/embedded-image/mold-link IDs, strips old import-batch lineage and approvals, and recalculates filled sections. Whole-product copying carries shared packaging, cartons and packaging labor once; component-only copying excludes them and automatically enables only departments that contain selected component rows or allocated electronic expenses, in addition to the mandatory or manually selected departments. Electronic shared expenses follow proportional raw-component-cost allocation. Old JustPlay quotes without component definitions expose an explicit legacy-main selection; its former product image becomes that selected new main component image. Each target product chooses `history_reference_mode=source|current`, defaulting to historical material/machine prices while using new commercial defaults and FX; conflicting source price tables fail visibly and require an explicit current-reference choice. Historical quick painting converted into detail rows retains direct labor/paint allocation (`cost_allocation=direct`, paint already includes 13% tax), including after frontend edits; ordinary detail rows retain their existing allocation. Source quotation, product, region, component, fingerprint, reference snapshot, section revisions and price mode are recorded in immutable history-reference audit evidence and displayed on the collaboration page. No source quotation is modified; imported new drafts must be checked for packaging, labor, shared electronic expenses and recalculation warnings before normal review.

The v3 collaboration form keeps calculation formulas on the server and omits formula-explanation rows from entry and summary surfaces. Painting detail entry supports nine HKD operation quantity/unit-price pairs: clamp, pad print, UV (`operations.uv`), spray, edge, paint, dip, wipe and PP water. The downloadable painting workbook follows the supplied template with H = UV quantity, I = UV unit price, V = operation total including H × I, W = 油漆, X = 人工 and Y = 备注. Header-based imports support relocated price/remark columns and legacy eight-operation sheets; missing UV defaults to zero. New rows and imports containing the oil/labor headers use cost_allocation=split with nullable paint_cost_hkd/labor_cost_hkd: the entered HKD costs classify directly as oil paint and spray labor, sum to the operation total, and never add that total again or add quick-quote paint tax. If exactly one amount is supplied, the other is the operation-total difference; both blank remain pending and block completed costing until filled, as the user plans to supply prices later. The same-row labor formula total-minus-paint is recomputed from operation inputs even without cached Excel results. Subtotal/total rows are excluded from imports. Frontend edits preserve nullable fields and component/markup metadata. Legacy sheets without these headers retain their 30% material/70% labor allocation, and historical cost_allocation=direct retains its already-taxed operation paint split. Ordinary, detached-multiplier and JustPlay exports use the same authoritative labor/material totals and pricing entries. Draft dirty-state checks, initial form values and whole-product conflict checks share the same frozen-reference material/machine canonicalization. Numeric preview formatting tolerates temporarily empty inputs, including the ordinary-customer paper-price factor, so clearing and retyping a number cannot blank the department form; this does not fill or persist a replacement business value. Equivalent section refreshes preserve locally edited rows and their input identity; saved payload acknowledgments update the baseline even without a new server revision, while edits made during a save stay dirty. Local drafts retain the revision they were based on when a conflicting refresh arrives, so later saves cannot silently bypass optimistic locking. Structured import requires saving local drafts first and a fresh preview if edits follow preview creation. Successful writes acknowledge the returned payload/revision even when subsequent reads fail; a quote-scoped warning offers read-only refresh, and batch readiness is refreshed after mutation. Equal payloads are still sent to the server for dependency/formula validity checks during whole-product saves. Quote HTTP requests use a scoped 120-second default and the proxy allows 150 seconds; network timeouts have unknown outcomes and must be reconciled by reading before retrying. Injection entry hides mold number, color, loss-inclusive weight and machine name while retaining compatible payload fields, uses the A-code selector for calculation, and shows live loss-inclusive weight, material-cost and molding-labor summaries. System-calculated monetary cells share the same green-highlight semantic. Assembly and packaging product groups accept a manual `total_persons` only while no process rows exist; adding the first process clears and locks that value to the live process-person sum, and deleting the final process clears it back to an editable empty value. The server calculator treats the manual total as authoritative only for groups without process rows and blocks groups whose applicable person total is not positive. Sales and engineering may edit the current product's dedicated material-price snapshot from the Quote Overview; saving creates a new frozen reference revision, recalculates every dependent section and invalidates affected approval or final-release state without changing the factory pricing baseline. Batch-product comparison returns granular field paths per changed department, but the continuous entry page reveals those details only while the user hovers or focuses the corresponding changed department.

Factory-scoped internal-quote customer records may be created, renamed and deleted by the local sales supervisor/manager or engineering supervisor/manager through `internal_quote:customer_manage`. The permission never expands operating access to another factory; ordinary sales staff and engineers remain read-only for this customer master unless separately authorized.

The domain uses optimistic revisions, immutable revision/audit records, frozen reference snapshots, server-side decimal calculations, dependency invalidation and controlled release. Quote mutations serialize per factory/batch (or quote for legacy standalone records) within the database transaction before checking current revisions: PostgreSQL uses transaction advisory locks and SQLite reserves the writer with BEGIN IMMEDIATE. This includes section saves, imports, attachment deletion, baseline copy and review transitions; no database migration is required for these locks. A final release or downstream handoff is no longer valid when its source revisions, reference snapshot, formulas or dependencies change.

Engineering mold imports and downloads use the fixed `展兴模具--工模报价表.xlsx` workbook. Numbered tax statements, payment/validity clauses and signature blocks without mold-detail inputs are excluded from structured imports and never inherit the preceding mold number. Corrected replacement imports remove obsolete Engineering-generated injection rows through the existing source synchronization while preserving surviving rows' molding inputs and manual injection rows. Mold child names remain independently editable. Engineering mold child-row entry shows name, color, net weight, output count and quantity; processing content and processing unit price are omitted from this table, while existing payload values remain compatible. Frontend initialization and backend export fallback share the same name rules: expand one unambiguous `左右` or `前后` pair, complete shared names in `前/后` and full-width-slash equivalents, split ordinary slash-separated names, and preserve parenthesized color/annotation text including slashes inside brackets. Re-splitting requires a preview and confirmation; exact-name matches and unique legacy bare-direction matches keep their entered fields, new rows start without copied prices/weights, and unmatched manual rows remain visible at the end for review. Saved child names take precedence over automatic name generation. Its current `01` sheet maps A–M as mold number, Chinese/item name, material type, cavities, sets, embedded picture, mold size, RMB mold price, mold-base material, daily capacity, machine type, net part weight and remark. The importer still accepts the preceding A–L revision where capacity and machine type share column J as `capacity/machine`, and F-column embedded pictures remain attached to their source mold row.

Structured internal-quote imports replace the data region mapped by their template, except that electronic imports can create or replace one independent electronic quotation. Electronic payloads may contain up to 20 quote_groups with stable IDs, names, independent parts/expenses/profit and source lineage; each group is calculated separately and all group totals enter the product total exactly once. The UI selects, renames, adds and removes groups, offers new or targeted-replacement imports, and keeps an empty state after the last group is removed. Legacy single payloads remain readable; ordinary saves cannot downgrade grouped data into the old single format. Final controlled exports show one visible electronic detail sheet per group while retaining the existing original-workbook attachment handling. Purchase formulas preserve each group's full allocation factor, and historical component reuse merges selections from the same source group with group-local expense allocation. Confirmed source workbooks are exposed as import-source attachments with durable batch lineage on imported rows. Deleting an import-source attachment is revision-locked and allowed only while its section is mutable; one transaction removes the source workbook, its generated rows and now-unreferenced embedded images, recalculates the section, invalidates downstream dependencies, marks the import batch deleted and writes revision/audit evidence. Deletion only removes rows carrying the selected import batch IDs; unmarked legacy/manual rows and newer import-owned scalar values are retained. Ordinary supporting attachments use a separate revision-checked delete endpoint and cannot be deleted while referenced by quote image fields. Baseline copying remaps image references to the newly copied attachment IDs in the destination quote.

Hair is a standalone optional internal-quote section. Its rows record name, craft, positive weight in grams, positive HKD unit price, unit and remark; the authoritative section total is the sum of HKD unit prices, never price multiplied by weight. Its download button serves the original fixed `车发部报价单.xls` unchanged. Hair imports accept that legacy XLS plus XLSX/XLSM, read the product HKD unit price and gram weight from the left-hand columns of the `明细` sheet (D/E in the fixed template), preserve source numeric precision, and never substitute the `正式` price or the right-hand material breakdown. Missing or invalid price/weight blocks structured import; formula cells must have saved calculated values. An empty product name falls back to the current quote product with a preview warning; missing craft/unit default to `车发`/`PCS`. Preview, confirmation, source retention, replacement and lineage deletion use the shared import workflow; other departments' structured imports still reject legacy XLS. Historical sewing payloads may still contain `category=hair`, but new forms use the standalone hair section and summaries prefer it whenever that section participates.

Engineering auxiliary-material rows and sales packaging-material rows accept either RMB or HKD as the entered unit-price currency. `unit_price_source_currency` identifies the authoritative input, the other unit price is derived from the quote's frozen RMB/HKD rate, and legacy rows without that field continue to prefer RMB unless they contain only an HKD price. Hardware rows remain RMB-input only. Hardware, auxiliary-material and sales packaging-material rows also carry a positive `loss_rate` multiplier that defaults to `1` for new and historical rows; the entered currency values remain the auditable supplier base price, while server-authoritative effective unit prices and amounts are calculated as base unit price × loss rate (for example `1.02`).

Internal-quote commercial multipliers do not add a separate workflow. For ordinary customers, non-molding detail rows inherit the Sales main multiplier unless they carry an explicit positive `markup_override`; an override equal to the main multiplier is normalized back to inheritance, while a different value is priced as a detached detail group and recombined in the summary/export. While an ordinary quote's Sales sidebar multiplier is being edited, the currently selected draft tier is previewed across department detail forms so inherited placeholders and override normalization match the sidebar before save; persistence still occurs only through the sidebar save action. The settlement divisor is always derived as `1 - misc_ratio`, so users edit only the miscellaneous rate. Huakang B JustPlay quotes enter component mode at creation and require a named component list per product. The create dialog keeps one main component plus 0-49 accessories inside each product card; adding a series product copies the current first product component names/count once, after which products are edited independently. The create API accepts products[].pricing_components, validates names within each product, and seeds that product own Sales payload and initial revision. Omitted product lists retain compatibility with the legacy top-level pricing_components, or inherit the first product list; explicit empty JustPlay lists are rejected. This changes new-quote setup only and does not rewrite existing quotes or change the separately requested whole-baseline-copy behavior. The collaboration page provides one product-scoped current-component selector above the continuous department form: applicable department forms show only that component's detail rows, and newly added rows inherit the selected component automatically, so a series quote follows batch → product → component. Engineering mold and Painting imports map the whole product once: the preview lists source rows and requires each row to be assigned to a current component or explicitly skipped, with batch selection available. Confirmation replaces the template-owned region once, retaining source lineage and revision validation. A product-wide assignment panel can subsequently change each mold/painting row's component without reimport; saving a mold assignment synchronizes the linked injection row while preserving the whole-mold fee once. Legacy unassigned rows keep their existing first-component fallback. Sales packaging materials, cartons, and assembly packaging/mixed-pack groups remain product-level shared items and stay visible for every component. For JustPlay/component-mode Sales, optional `pdq_size_in` dimensions appear below the color-box dimensions. Complete PDQ dimensions take priority: main-carton length/width/height = PDQ length/width/height + 0.75/0.75/1 inch. Without PDQ, `justplay_carton.dimension_source` selects `color_box` (legacy/default) or `product`, and each source dimension is multiplied by its corresponding positive-integer `length_count`/`width_count`/`height_count` before adding those allowances. Missing count fields default to 1. The three counts are shown in the color-box area, are disabled but retained while using PDQ, and never overwrite the independent product quantity per carton. Canonical storage remains inches; color-box, PDQ and carton display units can independently be cm or inch. The main carton dimensions are read-only, its name/packing quantity/flat cards remain editable, and inner cartons plus ordinary-customer cartons keep manual dimensions. A component-mode quote with carton data requires all three positive dimensions of the selected source. All-zero/absent PDQ means unused; a partially entered PDQ is rejected without falling back to another source. Without PDQ, blank, zero, negative or fractional directional counts are rejected. Old manual main-carton dimensions must never be a fallback. Existing quotes retain their stored history until explicitly saved/recalculated under normal revision and review rules. The derived dimensions feed carton cost, freight, adhesive and pallet capacity in both frontend and authoritative backend calculations. Component-mode Sales derives two product-level HKD/PCS costs from the first/main carton: `胶纸/胶水/胶针 = 3.9 ÷ 2150 × (length × 2 + width × 4 + 6) ÷ qty_per_carton + adhesive_extra_hkd` and `纸托板成本 = 19 ÷ cartons_per_pallet ÷ qty_per_carton + paper_pallet_extra_hkd`; length and width are canonical inches. Sales stores two editable additional amounts under `justplay_packaging`; both default to 0 and are rounded half-up to one decimal place in the form, current authoritative calculation and exported cost formulas. Saved payloads are not bulk rewritten; existing amounts adopt the current precision on normal save/recalculation. The same object stores editable `pallet_length_mm`, `pallet_width_mm` and `pallet_height_mm`, all finite and greater than zero, with millimeters as their fixed unit. Missing legacy fields default to length 1000, width 1150 and height 1300 mm to preserve the previous carton-axis mapping; explicit blank/zero/invalid fields never silently fall back. Cartons per pallet defaults to automatic calculation and accepts a manual positive integer in `justplay_packaging.cartons_per_pallet_override`. Missing/null override selects automatic mode; blank, zero, negative, fractional or nonfinite overrides are invalid. The form shows the effective count in an editable input and offers 恢复自动计算 to remove the override. Manual values survive dimension changes, save/reload and whole-product reuse, and drive both cost preview and authoritative/export cost calculations. Automatic capacity is computed as `floor(pallet_width_mm / (width_in * 25.4)) * floor(pallet_length_mm / (length_in * 25.4)) * floor(pallet_height_mm / (height_in * 25.4))`. Each axis is floored separately before multiplication, with no carton rotation. The displayed pallet dimensions are independent of carton inch/cm display preferences and never overwrite product quantity per carton. Whole-product historical reuse preserves the chosen product pallet parameters, while component-only reuse uses the new product shared packaging setup. Excel cost formulas retain the effective automatic or manually overridden capacity number and one-decimal additional amounts without expanding the pallet-capacity process into the output. Historical `cartons_per_pallet` values remain ignored; only the new explicit override activates manual mode. Missing dimensions show a completion warning, and dimensions yielding zero capacity block authoritative calculation unless a valid manual override is supplied; an empty carton list can remain a draft. The page hides both formulas and uses a separate JustPlay 胶纸及纸托板成本部分 with inputs/results outside the ordinary packaging-material table. The resulting rows remain automatic calculation evidence rather than editable `packaging_materials` payload rows and participate once in the shared business-packaging total. The right sidebar replaces MOQ multiplier editing with one multiplier per component plus an independent `业务部包装` multiplier. Component multipliers apply to their assigned detail rows; the business-packaging multiplier applies once to the shared packaging/carton/assembly-packaging cost and product-level additional tax, freight and lifting fees. Unassigned or obsolete component references fall back to the first component so historical data remains priceable, and historical JustPlay quotes without `shipping.packaging_markup_x` inherit the main component multiplier until the business-packaging multiplier is saved. JustPlay Sales also owns optional component-scoped `customer_supplied_materials` rows with item, `unit_price_hkd`, and `fee_rate_percent` (3 means 3%); fees are unit price × rate ÷ 100, rounded half-up to four HKD decimals per row. Zero price/rate is valid, but started rows require a name and finite nonnegative numbers, and rates cannot exceed 100%. Only the custody fee is included in cost/price; customer-owned material value is excluded. These rows follow the current-component picker, retain other components on edit, and follow their source component through historical quote reuse. Custody fees bypass every component/packaging multiplier and the miscellaneous/settlement divisor, do not qualify as RMB procurement or purchase-tax deductions, and enter final HKD/USD totals exactly once. Sales save and whole-quote preview subtract the previous stored fee from the base context before recalculating, including when all customer-supplied rows are deleted. Ordinary quotes reject nonempty customer-supplied rows.

Internal-quote formula contract `rr2-2026-v7` and workbook layout `internal-quote-unified-desk-v33` enforce current calculation and export versions, including JustPlay PDQ/product/color-box carton sources, directional packing counts, automatic pallet capacity, component images and separate department output blocks. JustPlay Excel places electronic, sewing and hair costs in separate nonempty blocks immediately below their owning component's primary detail block; each uses that component multiplier and the existing misc/fx formulas. Those amounts are excluded from the primary block and its residual adjustment, then every block is included once in total pricing and tax summaries. Nonempty customer-supplied material blocks follow their owning component and department blocks, showing fee formulas such as `=ROUND(0.8*0.03,4)`, the fee subtotal, frozen HKD/USD rate and USD custody fee. They deliberately omit multiplier/settlement rows and purchase-tax tags; final amount and miscellaneous formulas keep the fee separate from marked costs. JustPlay electronic blocks show IC, LED, speaker, PCB and battery separately. IC/LED/speaker use original imported component costs (quantity times unit price, converted by the frozen FX), including child rows, without the summary overhead allocation; Each imported electronic item, including PCB purchases and child rows, retains its own original unit-price/quantity formula; a PCB residual row subtracts all non-battery purchase details from the component electronic total excluding batteries. Engineering battery purchases move into the same component electronic block and are added once, retaining the battery summary category; battery holders/contacts remain in their original purchase category. Existing electronic-source battery lines are separated from the electronic total before re-adding them, so legacy data cannot double count batteries. The electronic source total is visible beside the block, and old workbook caches are invalidated by the layout version; authoritative section calculations are unchanged. Ordinary and JustPlay purchase details retain original supplier prices and quantities for hardware, electronics, auxiliary materials and Sales purchases. Their visible descriptions put specification before material name and append the actual quantity/unit (default pcs), including quantity one and electronic child lines; missing legacy quantities are not invented. RMB uses formulas such as `=0.52/$L$4*2`; HKD omits FX conversion, quantity one omits multiplication, and non-default losses and material cost allocations remain explicit factors. The exporter pairs original inputs with saved calculation rows in their full sequence, preserves entered-currency precedence and falls back to saved amounts when source identity is unavailable. Ordinary purchase categories expand into item rows, including detached multipliers; allocated electronic overhead moves with its detached item so the main row does not become negative. JustPlay global packaging purchases use visible arithmetic instead of links to hidden detail cells. A component image is proportionally fitted beside its own molding/primary detail area; ordinary-customer layout and product-image behavior remain unchanged. Summary reports the running formula version; whole-review submission/review and controlled export reject quotes or filled sections calculated by an older formula. Authorized Sales or Engineering reference managers can create a new frozen reference revision with unchanged reference values and recalculate every participating filled section under the current formula; this invalidates affected approvals and artifacts. Final-export cache reuse also requires the current formula, reference snapshot, header revision, section revisions and calculation hashes, so a matching release number alone cannot return an obsolete workbook. The continuous collaboration page registers every dirty department and requests one server-side whole-quote preview; the sidebar uses its exact component/packaging pricing groups instead of assigning the total delta to the first component. Whole-product save validates all optimistic revisions first and persists all supplied departments in one transaction, including Engineering-to-Molding dependency updates, so any calculation failure rolls the complete save back. After a rejected v3 review, changing customer or quantity validates ordinary-versus-Huakang-B-JustPlay component compatibility and recalculates every filled participating section before the quote can be submitted again.

Sales flat-card rows carry an explicit positive quantity. Frontend preview and server-authoritative calculation both use `length_in × width_in × flat_card_price_factor × quantity ÷ 1000`; the configured flat-card factor defaults to the carton paper-price factor, and legacy rows without quantity default to `1`.

The controlled internal-quote `.xlsx` export is formula-driven and named `报价号_产品名_YYYY-MM-DD.xlsx`. Its molding detail links material price, molding labor and material amount to the frozen material/machine references and line inputs; carton output inserts every configured inner-carton dimension row and, when inner cartons exist, shows separate outer/inner per-piece prices plus an inner/outer packing-quantity cell (for example 6/24). Each carton formula divides by its own packing quantity and references its own main/inner price factor. The left detail lists inner cartons before the outer carton and links directly to these visible results. Flat-card cost is allocated using the owning carton quantity and included exactly once; a single-carton layout keeps box-level prices and divides their sum by its packing quantity. JustPlay uses the same split carton rows in the shared packaging block. Its main-carton dimensions retain formulas referring to the selected visible PDQ/product/color-box cells, directional-count cells when no PDQ is used, and the 0.75/0.75/1 inch allowances with independent input/carton unit conversions. The PDQ row appears directly below color-box dimensions only when used; without PDQ the carton area displays the selected source and three directional counts. The additional rows participate in the existing dynamic layout, formula translation and borders. Visible formulas are translated to the new position instead of hidden-sheet links; centimetre inputs are converted to inches in both carton and adhesive formulas. Ordinary detached-multiplier rows remain in the original quotation flow as category/detail lines followed by their own cost, multiplier, settlement and quote rows; the original bottom management summary aggregates both the main-detail range and every detached range directly with bounded `SUMIF` formulas, without adding a separate segmented-summary table. JustPlay exports follow the same pattern per component, render packaging/carton once with the independent business-packaging multiplier, add the automatic `胶纸/胶水/胶针` and `纸托板成本` rows to the visible packaging detail, and retain the cost arithmetic as real Excel formulas in their amount cells, referencing the visible main-carton dimensions and outer-carton packing quantity. The two user-entered additional amounts are embedded as two-decimal numeric constants; the pallet-cost formula embeds the server-calculated cartons-per-pallet count as an integer. The workbook does not render an additional parameter block or the pallet-capacity calculation process. That capacity is an export-time result: changing carton dimensions in an exported workbook does not recalculate the embedded count; re-export from the application to obtain an updated count. They add enabled freight/lifting once in the final product-quote block using that multiplier and perform the HKD/USD conversion only once after the component and packaging HKD quotes are combined; the original management summary directly aggregates all visible component and packaging detail ranges. Only explicitly enabled freight/lifting routes and MOQ tiers are rendered, and disabling testing fees removes the testing block instead of leaving an empty frame. The carton panel shows CBM as a formula linked to its visible CUFT, which references outer-carton dimensions with inch/cm conversion. Ordinary and JustPlay transport blocks expose frozen capacity and whole-container fees; carton count is MAX(1, ROUND(capacity / CUFT, 0)), and enabled per-piece fees use ROUND(whole fee / carton count / outer-carton quantity, 4). Editing the exported dimensions or packing quantity updates these formulas and downstream quoted prices; JustPlay transport tax summaries link the same visible fee rows. Source routes must match the frozen summary and explicitly separate freight/lifting with complete enabled fee inputs; older combined-price or incomplete snapshots retain their frozen allocated fees instead of inventing source totals. The separate right-side color-box table is BuzzBee-only; other customers retain color-box/internal-card cost in the main detail summary. The RMB-purchase and 13%-tax summary cells use the visible detail ranges' tax-tag columns as bounded `SUMIF` sources: the 13% cell totals `¥13%`, while RMB purchases equal that cell plus detail rows tagged `¥1%`. Sewing download uses the fixed `车缝报价单.xlsx` RMB-input template with a per-row RMB/HKD exchange-rate column. Sewing imports preserve unit-price currency: An explicit row currency or unit-price currency takes precedence; otherwise rate-column templates use RMB. Without a rate column, an explicit total-price currency is respected, and unlabelled legacy sheets use HKD. HKD source prices are retained at full imported precision and calculate usage × HKD price × markup without another FX division; RMB rows retain the existing conversion. Persisted legacy payloads without a currency marker keep RMB semantics and are never silently migrated. The sewing form and exported detail display the original currency, and Excel formulas use it.

Customer-price artifacts are derived from approved structured quote data through customer-specific converters. Factory-scoped customer masters and pricing baselines remain separate from the internal collaboration state. The pricing-baseline editor discards completely blank material, machine and freight rows before validation; an automatically generated freight route key does not make a row nonblank. Started freight rows require name, capacity and explicit nonnegative freight/lifting amounts (zero is valid), with at least one route retained; validation selects the freight tab and scrolls/focuses the first invalid field. BuzzBee, Disney, Dickie, Caixing and the temporary Yinhui/Silverlit converter are Huaxing-only mappings; the Huakang A customer-order mappings for 360, Green Toys and HeadStart are factory-specific and do not share parsers or exporters with same-name profiles elsewhere. Huakang B/C/D and Huadeng expose independent unmapped states until an explicit factory-specific mapping chain is added.
