Customer-facing outputs must not expose internal commercial fields outside the converter whitelist. 客价转换基础信息按厂区/客户保存在服务端，包含 BuzzBee、银辉料价及各已映射客户实际使用的分类倍率/费率；彩星保留来源材料成本，不增加未使用的料价表，Dickie 维护料价只改报客展示、不重算已审核方案价。查看/维护分别使用 `customer_price:settings_read` / `customer_price:settings_manage`：业务人员可看，业务主管/经理可维护；旧版业务负责人/业务主管角色在启动时一次性补齐对应权限并更新授权版本，保留用户明确拒绝、原厂区/部门绑定及补齐后的人工撤权；普通工程/生产及跨厂只读不获得访问，前后端均校验业务身份、厂区范围和明确拒绝，系统全局管理员仍遵循既有权限政策。默认资料仅随后端打包（`shared/customerPriceDefaults.json`），不作为前端静态资源。基础信息维护区使用独立客户下拉框，只列当前厂区已映射客户，切换维护客户只读取资料，不切换当前报价、不重新转换或叠乘倍率；输出区客户切换同样不改已有转换结果。页面不展示写死的客户单数、待转换/待复核统计或固定车间/负责人。保存须匹配 revision；每次转换冻结不可修改的服务端参数快照，客表只携带编号，通用审计也只记标识/版本。保存新参数后，当前报价须恢复转换预览或重新导入才采用新参数，重复导出沿用当前转换快照。Dickie 合并允许同范围、同 revision、同内容的不同快照并保留全部编号。BuzzBee 新流程由新建报价确定产品名、版本、日期，每方案独立；P4 传递新建日期，旧放行文件缺日期需重新导出。料重为整套重量，料金额按重量×维护的公斤价÷1000×料价倍率，啤工按内部计算啤工×客户倍率；普通外购/车缝默认1.05、纸箱1.03，其他新增倍率默认1，不与旧固定倍率叠乘。电子按报价整组输出，子弹为附加件、子弹包装为普通明细；彩盒整箱价除装箱数并始终另报，FSC 由维护倍率计算。主明细 SUB 后加 P+O（默认10%），TOTAL四舍五入2位，再加附加件，USD按维护汇率（默认7.75）；BuzzBee不计算运费/测试费。业务选择六种已净化原版版式，统一取消黄色底色填充，保留样本其余颜色、字体、列宽、边框、说明区和图片锚点；额外行扩展并更新公式引用。特殊料型通过明确客户料价名称对应，不能根据产品名猜测。

华兴 Dickie / Dicky 内部报价新增显式启用的 `dickie-v2` 补录，保存在业务 `customer_quote_fields.dickie.mapping` 与工程模具逐行 `dickie_export`；不迁移旧客价字段，也不改变银辉临时映射。通用业务保存的英寸尺寸与主纸箱装箱数自动带入，支持选择产品/彩盒、cm/mm 和仅影响报客展示的外箱尺寸；中英文产品名、变动说明、抬头、方案名称/备注及模期分别录入。每个报价方案选择参与输出的 MOQ 档位和 40柜/20柜/散货三条路线；正常价由服务端复用成本汇总计算全部档位，按内部报价口径加入已启用且匹配 MOQ 的测试费（已计算 USD 单件费 × 档位倍率 × 冻结汇率），再写入 P4 `customer_mapping` 分片，不在浏览器重算成本。已确认价须三价完整且有内部依据，支持有原因的顺序百分比/定额调整，基础与每步结果四舍五入至一位小数。固定不含5%折扣、测试标准、未含吊柜/入仓费、报客料价（默认 PP4.8/ABS5.8/C-ABS8.5/HIPS5.1 HKD/LB，可在客户基础信息中维护）、涨价保留权、来板法律责任及未付清拥有权条款自动输出；所选已计算价含吊柜费时阻断。模具报价可不输出；输出时复用工程模号/中文名称/胶料/模具尺寸/模料/穴数/套数，补英文、分组/共用产品、明确报客 HKD 模价和双语模期，不从内部成本推断售价。主流程为内部报价台填写、整单审核放行后进入交接池，客价转换台接收并转换；Dickie 历史 Excel 兼容入口默认收起。新 P4 接收与历史文件兼容均识别新版，预览按产品/MOQ/三种运输单价显示，输出仅包含 `Quotation` 和 `总表` 两页及主产品图；新版导出代码固化用户提供的 Revell 32257 样板样式（`dickieTemplateStyles.json` 只存原样板字体、颜色、边框、行高、列宽和主题，不含样例数据），保留浅绿红字抬头、蓝色下划线标题、A:K 列序、K 列图片、黄色折扣提示及 B:C/E:F 两组料价表；方案和模具行按同样样式动态扩展，数值与内部成本隔离。旧 Dickie 专用 Excel 转换继续保留。客价台可将多个新版产品加入合并清单，同一抬头、日期、版本和报价类型才能合并；移除/清空清单会作废合并预览，切厂清空清单。内部工作簿布局版本为 v32，以便导出新增的客户价格分片；旧放行文件缺分片须重新走适用的编辑、审核和导出流程。

银辉客价使用独立临时映射，接受银辉原内部 `.xlsx` 或华兴最终放行 P4 文件。原表按内部型号选择用户提供的机器人标准版、81283 活动环、88636 Rescue Bear、89115 Pee Pee Puppy、89275 Kneading Kitten、89127 MUSCLE CLASH 开窗盒、88753 Goal Go Bot 礼盒七种六表版式；去样例数据的模板存为 `public/templates/yinhui-*.bin`，定义集中在 `yinhuiProfiles.ts`。输出保留各模板字体、边框、合并格、行高、列宽及打印设置；除已确认的总表出厂价公式 `J32=J30-J31+H32` 和 89275 五金区扩行所需的引用顺延外，其余原公式（含共享公式）保持不变，仅填映射输入并刷新缓存，不复制旧产品资料、供应商页或内部采购信息。BOM 区块和 Tool Plan 总计行按模板标题定位，包装 I33:I34 以及 88753 的 J33:J34 合并格必须跳过内部行。88753 使用所提供礼盒版式的 37 行五金、18 行电子及双 Battery 资料行；89275 五金区以原明细行样式扩至 13 行，所属小计、跨表公式和打印范围同步延伸，不能合并不同物料以挤入旧容量。AAA/AA 正片、负片和正负片按原行分别保留数量与金额，不再汇成电池片套装；PC/PVC 片归 MECHANICAL MAT'L。报关/文件费单独汇入 SUM H32，从包装成本剔除，仅由出厂价公式计入一次，保留内部倍率计算精度，不复制手填凑整金额。资料区按 Battery/Adaptor/Try me 标题定位，不能固定旧行号。 银辉原表和 P4 的 BOM 成本明细保留来源中文及规格，不再经词库或模型改成英文；核对区显示原文，中文不阻止导出，空白和 Excel 错误名称仍须补全。彩盒识别兼容中英文，保持尺寸和明细行对应。银辉导入后在核对组件中仅自动翻译产品名、包装方式及 Tool Plan 名称，使用工厂限定为华兴、客户限定为银辉且需客价导入权限的 `/api/pricing/translate-descriptions`。接口只接收名称文本，不接收报价文件、金额或价格；复用现有 `ctranslate2`/`sentencepiece` 离线模型与术语词库，不调用外部服务、不持久化文本。完整未知中文短语才交模型，原英文/数字/规格片段保持不变（仅整理多余空格）；服务端拒绝模型新增数字，前端再校验规格与数量文本。保留翻译前描述供对照，翻译中撤销导出确认；失败保留原文并允许重试或手工修改，替换导入、切换工厂和离开页面会取消旧请求，迟到结果不得覆盖手工修改。缺 C-PP/LDPE 等客料价与翻译无关，仍沿用缺料价确认导出规则。单 BOM 汇入第一组，第二组不填明细，不推断 RX/TX；原表无独立 Tool Plan 时按明细模组拆件，首件承接组总料重及啤工。完整匹配的模具报价按逐件模穴数及每啤成品数确定出模量和用量，先验证模穴合计，不能把“1出几套”直接当作单件模穴；不重复乘总料重和啤工。缺少依据的客户模号/零件号留空，模具费用须匹配当前型号和零件。89127 必须唯一选择开窗盒页，不能默认读第一张密封盒页；同列重复外箱尺寸按 2.54 单位关系交叉核对，并列方案（如 89275 内部/客人两栏）按当前纸箱成本公式依赖选择完整尺寸、装箱数和 MOQ，无法唯一判断则阻断。原表先扫描各 Sheet 前 80 行发现来源，再完整读取所需页的有值单元格及公式依赖。银辉原表、草稿恢复及 AI 候选共用页名和表头识别：兼容空格、大小写、全半角、常见繁体和附加符号，支持明细页名后缀；陌生页名须通过名称/料型/料重列、型号标题及内部出厂价结构识别，仍保留原始页名和单元格地址作为补正、草稿、图片及 AI 来源。除既定 89127 开窗盒规则外，多张候选明细、Tool Plan 或规范化后同名模具报价页必须列出原名并阻断，不能自动任选或合并费用。86889 对战恐龙原表的“明细 ”和“TOOL  PLAN +”可直接进入草稿，宽列 Tool Plan 优先读取明确的 HK 模号与 Net 单件净重，不能误用大陆模号或每啤胶重；不明确的对应仍须人工核对。该表 L18:L22 位于“啤工”列却用 F×E 计算料金额，与 J 列啤工按倍率换算冲突，五项费用问题继续阻止正式导出，不能靠模糊匹配关闭金额校验。此识别兼容已在本地验证，尚未部署到线上。Tool Plan 识别成功时兼容经典 A:M 与紧凑 B:N 布局：经典 `Qty/Toy` 允许塞模行用量为 0，紧凑 `/ up` 的逐件用量等于模穴数除以每啤成品数；同一物理模具内胶料切换时按料型分段对主表逐组核重，但客户模号只写一次。Tool Plan 行格式异常、无法与主明细唯一匹配或存在未匹配分段时不阻断导入：数字文本按数值接受，缺零件号保留空白；缺描述、公式错误、仅模号分组、模号/料型分组不明确及无效数值必须逐行提醒，不能静默遗漏；无法匹配时改用主明细的料重、料型、啤工和出模套数生成模号/零件号留空的临时行，未匹配分段不自动写入，逐项列出原因并提示金额可能受影响，用户人工核对后可明确确认放行。模具报价编号与已识别 Tool Plan 不同只能在当前型号兼容且首件描述唯一时回退匹配，并明确提示复核；不能用模糊描述吞掉未匹配模具费。原表车缝总价映射到 FABRIC COST；电子/车缝描述括号内的产品型号以及其他描述尾部无单位的四位以上括号数字不属于用量，外箱与防割板累加。独立文件费只能由明确标签且未进入倍率成本区的行识别，出厂价上一行小计不得猜成文件费；最终报价区的 FCL/LCL 也不得覆盖盐田柜/散单件运费。

银辉映射须交叉核对六组历史内部/报客样本及新增 88753 更正资料，不能视第一份或任一手填客表为绝对标准。版式按型号保留，塑料 HKD/kg 全型号统一：ABS 15.65、TPR 18.8、PP 12.6、POM 34.07、C-ABS 24 采用客户提供的价表；PVC 17.16、TPE 15.9 由内部料价与样表交叉核对补足。PP 12.62/PVC 17 等旧表差异属于口径选择，不能一概宣称人工填错；当前统一价表不随 SKU 改变。以内部实际料型和总料重计算，不复制样表误填标签（81283 PVC 写成 ABS、89275 PP 写成 POM）。HKD/USD 为 7.8。原表其余成本统一按当前内价×所属区间倍率÷结算除数计算；完整报客金额列只用于校验，差异超过 HKD 0.005 阻断，零散备注/内价别名不作为价格。没有原啤工但有完整已报价啤工时才允许回退。不复制参考客表旧价、手工凑整或 MOQ。P4 仍仅支持已覆盖的审批 calculation 类型，车缝/搪胶/植发等正金额和无确认摊分规则的费用会阻断，不能将原表车缝支持等同于 P4 全部支持。型号、MOQ、产品及 Tool Plan 英文名称、BOM 原文、料型、模具费、图片与 LCL/40FT 每件运费必须在输出前核对；修改资料或替换文件会撤销确认。未配置银辉报客料价的料型保留原名称，价格查询返回空值；页面列出缺价料型并把合计标为已知成本小计，用户明确知悉单价留空、合计不完整后仍可导出，导出函数也要求显式缺价确认。对应 Tool Plan 港币公斤价单元格留空，原公式照常保留，不能套用内价、其他客户价或写入零单价；文件名增加“待补料价”，SUM 的 STAGE 保留原阶段并标注 PRICE PENDING，补齐料价后才发送客户。银辉原表导入采用先保留草稿、集中补正、重新核对后输出的流程；MOQ 支持 5K、5,000、千/万等明确正整数写法，空白或歧义值保持待补正。可识别文件的料型/金额缺失、公式缓存错误、未知成本、倍率与报客金额冲突、无效尺寸/运费、模具费用错误及容量超限在草稿集中列出，未解决前导出函数和页面均阻止正式输出，不能通过勾选确认绕过；未识别成本只从已识别小计暂缺，必须明确标为不完整草稿。补正保留原文件及原始成本行身份，不能清空分类、名称、价格校验值或注塑输入来跳过费用/交叉核对；每次补正从原文件重建，并撤销输出确认和下方手工资料。模具候选识别兼容靠后表头及中英双语列，人工选择只补模号/零件号，不覆盖总料重、用量、啤工和费用。模具报价按业务行而非金额类型识别，缺价、错误和负价逐行保留阻断，数值文本按金额接受。草稿保存为包含原表及补正记录的内部 JSON 文件，从银辉导入口重开时校验华兴/银辉标识、重新解析并重放限定补正，只恢复允许编辑的资料和名称，不信任保存的金额、问题清单或放行状态；无效 MOQ 重开仍须补正。新草稿标记 `bomLanguage: source` 并恢复人工核对的 BOM 名称；无此标记的旧草稿重新采用原表解析的 BOM 中文，不恢复旧自动英文名称，Tool Plan 名称和其余可编辑资料仍按原规则恢复。草稿不是客户报价，缺客料价仍只允许显式确认后的“待补料价/PRICE PENDING”文件。P4 审批数据不接受源值补正，原审批/客户/厂区检查继续执行；无法识别客户、核心表头或不支持的 P4 仍拒绝导入。替换导入开始立即撤销银辉确认，导入中禁止导出旧结果。81283 和 89127 原客表料重公式内含固定常量；G×2 类公式反推输入单重，所有逐行总料重必须与内部一致，不能擅改固定公式来迁就新料重。各参考模板 LOCAL DELIVERY / FOB YT 20FT 两行原有除零公式继续保留并提示；机器人原样本还存在文件名 88538 与明细 88528、MOQ 及模具页归属冲突；88753 参考文件名写为 88573、参考 MOQ 为 5,000，而内部型号为 88753、MOQ 为 50,000，以当前内部资料为准，不能静默使用参考资料覆盖当前内部数据。

银辉原表草稿提供用户主动触发的 AI 辅助识别，复用现有文档工具的千问连接（服务端密钥、布局模型和超时），独立走 /api/pricing/yinhui-recognition 及其 /status 接口，限定华兴/银辉和客价导入权限，不恢复已退役的通用 AI 子系统。只发送相关表头、部件名称及已有候选编号，不发送整份文件或读取金额字段；模型只能选择给定候选或明确无法判断，服务端校验任务数量/顺序/候选并从输入生成来源。建议显示原单元格和理由，必须逐项确认：模具对应只补模号/零件号，Tool Plan 表头映射只扩展资料候选且随草稿保存，未知成本类别只补分类后重新解析并保留金额交叉核对。支持的主表选页与转换器共用规则，包括内部明细和 89127 开窗盒；替换文件、手工编辑、禁用和离页会作废请求，迟到结果不得覆盖草稿。最多 40 个任务、每项 80 个候选及总文字限额，超限项留待手工核对。缺连接、超时、输出不完整或有歧义时继续手工流程，不自动计算或补齐缺失价格。当前本地没有配置千问密钥，真实模型效果仍需在已配置环境验收；本地修改不代表已部署。

彩星业务专属字段保存塑胶/毛绒类型、Item No.、Item Description、报价日期和可选的本单加价比例；外箱长宽高、CUFT、CBM、Pcs/Shipper 与纸箱价统一读取业务第一条主纸箱及其服务端计算行，客户模板成本行统一读取各已审批分段的 calculation，不允许在彩星区维护重复副本。Tool Plan 可填内部料价 HKD/lb 和啤机日价，由客户参数按磅转公斤、料价上浮、料重损耗及每日小时/有效工时换算客户料金额和啤工；也可唯一匹配已审批的内部啤机明细，匹配不到时沿用已填客户金额并提示核对，CP/DC/RC 不自动匹配注塑价。客户设置分开维护塑胶/毛绒、电子、特殊料、外购、包装和车衣料/工的损耗与加价、汇率、运费和 MOQ，转换使用冻结快照、每类倍率只乘一次。客价输出按客户模板口径自动重分配：Tool Plan 的 `BL` 吹气工序成本不计入 `Molding & Casting`，而计入 `Summary` 的 `Spraying`；油漆和喷油人工合计计入 `Tampo Printing`。电池计入 `Purchase`，`IC` 计入 `Special Material`，利宝/说明书、锡线、胶针和胶纸计入 `Packing`。两套彩星模板的 `Packing` B 列是固定类型目录，转换器必须保留原值，只在匹配类型行的 C 列及其后写入规格、数量和成本。塑胶 Tool Plan 连同历史吹气/搪胶附加工序最多扩至 100 行，车衣模板限 51 行，超限阻断；原模板样式与图片锚点保留，用本单产品图片替换样本图。旧内部 Excel 的料型与料价公式引用不一致（如 68972 的 ABS 引用 PVC 价）只提示人工核对，不写入彩星倍率。

360 业务专属字段只保存 MS Brand、製表人、发行日期、版本、首次货柜出货日期和最终运费路线；MOQ、彩盒/纸箱尺寸、CUFT、Pcs/Carton、纸箱价、测试费和全部成本均复用通用业务字段及服务端 calculation，不保存重复副本。华康 A / 360 导入自动识别 P4 v2 最终放行 `.xlsx` 与原内部多 Sheet `.xlsx`；原内部文件只要求“内部明细”页，日常导入不要求也不应依赖客户输出页 `Breakdown`，彩盒、车衣等页可随源文件保留但不会复制到客户文件。原内部文件缺少客户抬头元数据时，製表人回退郑大能、发行日期优先从文件名读取、MOQ 使用当前 360 报价基数 20,000；旧 `.xls` 继续阻断。输出只生成客户 `Breakdown` 页，不携带内部明细、供应商分解页、样例产品图、外部链接或共享字符串残留。塑胶材料按客户固定 USD/KG 表（ABS 2.03、C-ABS 3.53、PP 1.70、PVC 1.92、C-PVC 2.26、POM 3.10、Roto-PVC 2.18、C-PP 1.84）乘实际含损耗重量；港币成本按 7.8 换算美元，材料损耗固定 2%，Markup 固定 12%。车衣快捷总价因无法逐项输出用量和单价而必须阻断；未配置的塑胶材料名、缺纸箱或缺运费路线同样阻断。

The pricing API persists factory- and customer-scoped pricing quotes. Totals are recalculated server-side and tampered client totals are rejected.

### Customer Order Center

Cancelled ledger orders can be restored on the original line through `POST /api/customer-order-ledger/lines/{id}/restore`, with current revision, a 4–500-character reason and explicit confirmation. Restoration changes status only, appends an immutable active version and preserves identities, original files, customer fields, historical openings and current shipment/reversal evidence; remaining quantity is recalculated from current facts, not an old version. Unknown quantities stay unknown and fully shipped orders stay completed. Factory-scoped write permission is required; previously sent orders also require dispatch permission and acknowledgement of notifying all original recipients with a new whitelisted snapshot. Recipient permission is checked under the line lock; on denial the business transaction is rolled back before the shared authorization helper commits its denial audit. Unsent restorations do not dispatch. Cancellation and restoration have identity-specific second confirmations in the shared ledger/schedule detail, with busy guards and stale-version refresh; no production tasks or inventory are automatically reactivated. This source implementation does not establish that a live cancelled record has been restored or that the feature has been released to production.

Customer-order preview, export, ledger import and history parsing now use a disposable file-processing process rather than the API thread pool. A request-wide 240-second budget, a nonblocking per-process gate plus a worker-owned per-container file lock, Linux 2 GiB address-space/CPU limits and a worker watchdog bound heavy Excel/native-PDF/OCR work; disconnects or cancellation reap the owned process group including nested OCR and converters. Authorization reads release their DB connection before file computation; business validation, fingerprint checks and atomic writes remain in the API after success. Output IPC is limited to 128 MiB. The existing sequential OCR, one-thread and page-render protections remain inside this boundary. Linux retains waitid/WNOWAIT and the 2 GiB address-space limit; macOS observes kqueue NOTE_EXIT without reaping the leader and skips the unsupported address-space limit, while retaining watchdog/CPU/cleanup budgets. These compatibility changes affect shared file-processing flows, not only MAXX; production-server MAXX failure still requires the actual failing files and server error evidence. Workbook insertion moves only existing cells, preserving distant user content without materializing its rectangular extent; existing formula/merge/validation adjustments remain authoritative. Ledger import/history file endpoints have a dedicated 270-second proxy timeout, while ordinary endpoints retain their short limits. The retired carton ACCESS editor no longer causes all-employee IAM resolution on master-data reads; canonical role checks and legacy provenance remain unchanged. Multi-replica distributed admission is not implemented. Abrupt supervisor death may leave a bounded worker temporary directory; orphan-directory retention remains separate maintenance. This process boundary is part of the tracked production release; future source releases must preserve it.

客户订单中心顶部“使用教程”在桌面与移动端均可打开，覆盖导入核对、异常处理、保存与排期下载、发送、分批走货及历史迁入；教程强调 Excel 导出与台账保存是独立操作，订单中心不维护生产排产。

客户订单中心是模块反馈试点，入口包括顶部“反馈问题”、侧栏/移动端“反馈与答复”、导入错误及订单详情。共用 `src/features/module-feedback` 与 `/api/module-feedback`，目前只开放 `customer-order-center`。用户可提交文字、表情、上传/粘贴截图和附件；截图支持画框、箭头、画笔、文字、裁剪及不透明遮挡，保存后仅发送编辑后的图片。浏览器支持时可主动授权截图。页面、厂区、订单标识、文件名等上下文随草稿冻结；原始业务文件须主动添加，不能自动上传。附件限定 PNG/JPG/WebP/PDF/XLS/XLSX，每次最多 5 个、单个 10 MB、合计 25 MB，服务端验证内容并经受保护接口读取。

运行时员工身份已确认且归属具体厂区的在用账号，均可在所属厂区提交及查看本人反馈，无须业务角色或订单查看权限；未确认、集团归属及其他厂区不能以此资格提交。`module_feedback:submit` 用于独立禁止或禁用反馈，明确允许也不能扩大员工所属厂区；业务数据权限的拒绝不阻止反馈权限问题。开发处理须显式 `module_feedback:manage`（厂区/系统部门范围），不随普通业务职位或总经理业务权限自动授予。每种授权模式均校验范围与明确拒绝，职位的跨厂范围不自动扩展反馈提交或订单关联。能力接口 `can_link_order` 独立校验本厂规范 `customer_order:read`，保留同厂内置职位的部门语义。无订单查看权限时前端只自动关联页面/分区/应用版本，用户仍可主动描述问题及添加资料；服务端在查询前拒绝携带订单 ID 的请求，撤销订单查看权限后也隐藏作者旧反馈中自动派生的订单字段，保存的证据保持原样。开发者点选回复、开始处理、请求资料、上线邀请验证后明确发送；上线邀请必须有实际环境/版本或修复说明。状态为已提交、待补充、处理中、待验证、已解决；资料补齐自动转处理中，只有反馈作者能确认解决或重新打开。回复支持仅附件；并发修改校验 revision，网络结果未知时保留原请求进行幂等重试。用户/开发者未读独立统计，只按当前可见对话版本记录已读，页面在前台每 30 秒检查更新。反馈消息、附件、已读凭据为只追加证据。迁移 `20261005_0120` 新增四表及证据保护；已有反馈时禁止丢弃数据的降级。前向汇合迁移 `20261005_0131` 将反馈分支与 main 的 `20260929_0130` 汇合，既有迁移不重写。本地业务 SQLite 当前记录版本为 `20261008_0146`，模块反馈四表、索引及只追加证据保护与原迁移一致并可正常启动。生产发布尚未执行。

The persisted order ledger is a separate `/api/customer-order-ledger` domain with eight `order_ledger_*` tables introduced by migration `20260914_0112`. It reuses the existing preview/manual-confirmation/export engines without changing customer PO or workbook mapping rules. Explicit save reparses the uploaded originals on the server, verifies the preview fingerprint and resolutions, preserves public customer extensions/source files, and writes an atomic batch. Factory/customer/business identities prevent duplicate lines; EDU uses customer contract/PO plus product rather than workbook-allocated EDUHX references. Single-line formal PO reconciliation explicitly preserves the original line ID, prior identities, shipments and immutable versions. Quantity/date/remark amendments and cancellation are revision-checked; sent-order changes publish new immutable snapshots to the original recipients. Quantity amendments invalidate stored derived amounts/cartons; delivery-date amendments invalidate related inspection/FCD/date-code values and carry a recheck notice rather than applying guessed customer formulas. The order ledger and factory schedule use persisted data with pagination and unshipped/shipped/cancelled filters. The dedicated 各客排期 view at `/api/customer-order-ledger/schedule` requires an explicit factory-owned customer and reads the same ledger; keyword/delivery-date filters apply before exact-decimal summaries, undated orders are excluded when either date bound is used, and partial deliveries appear in both independently paginated unshipped/shipped sections while counting once in totals. Cancelled remaining quantities are zero and unknown quantities are flagged. Customer columns come from an allowlist of actually saved order facts and remain stable across filters/pages; historical raw columns remain in detail. Shared detail actions refresh this view without duplicating order records or changing PO mappings. Dashboard/exception pages remain labeled preview aids. Saving orders and exporting Excel remain separate actions; importing a PO template does not automatically migrate its historical rows. The independent `/api/customer-order-ledger/history` preview/confirm flow reads named common headers from ITEM/category sheets, cross-checks review rows, preserves original files/columns/coordinates, blocks ambiguous identities and invalid/formula-cache rows, and requires explicit selected statuses, exact opening shipped quantities, cutoff date and reason. Historical openings live in `data.history_migration` backed by immutable versions; they do not create shipping documents or automatically dispatch. Same-file selected-row retries are idempotent; existing business identities cannot be overwritten. Opening corrections retain evidence and later shipments; post-cutoff shipping dates prevent double counting. Formal PO reconciliation preserves opening evidence. Dispatch snapshots carry shipped/remaining quantities and historical cutoff, clearly presented as quantities at sending time.

Downstream order inboxes at `/modules/pmc-warehouse/customer-order-inbox` and `/modules/production/customer-order-inbox` receive whitelisted order facts, including customer carton-mark/label requirements but excluding prices, source downloads and parser internals. Sending is unique per line/version/recipient and receipt is idempotent; this does not generate carton-procurement lines or injection demands. Shipment confirmation uses exact decimal quantities, optimistic revision checks, an idempotency key and shipping-document checks; over-shipment is rejected, full shipment leaves the unshipped view, and reversal restores the remaining quantity without deleting the original event. Permissions `customer_order:write`, `dispatch`, `shipment_confirm`, `inbox_read`, `inbox_receive` are canonical and department/factory scoped. Fixed Sales/warehouse/molding roles include their respective new actions under catalog `fixed-v27`; explicit denies and scope restrictions remain authoritative. Merge head `20260914_0116` joins the ledger revision `20260914_0112` and UV revision `20260914_0115` without rewriting either migration branch. The separate `D:/RR/royal-regent-nexus` business SQLite is at merged revision `20260914_0116`, including all eight ledger tables, 14 SQLite evidence-protection triggers and the UV schema. Backed-up/restored-copy rehearsal and the actual upgrade preserved the data and schema of all 196 pre-existing business tables; backend startup and health checks pass. Repair evidence is retained under `D:/RR/maintenance-backups/order-ledger-local-repair-20260914/`. The active checkout SQLite at `D:/royal regent heyuan/Royal-Regent-Nexus/backend/data/royal_regent_nexus.db` is also at merge head `20260914_0116`, including both order-ledger and UV schemas. Production ledger migration/release and historical schedule cutover require separate execution. Warehouse auto-feedback, returns, over-delivery, short-close rules and dedicated multiple-delivery allocation remain outside this first implementation.

The implemented backend capability supports factory-owned customer mappings in Huaxing, Huadeng, Huakang A and Huakang D, with Huakang C retained only for historical API compatibility. The import flow requires selecting a customer before files can be chosen, and the server rejects a customer mapping when the submitted factory does not own that customer. Every Customer Order Center list, summary/KPI, customer filter and recent-activity view is factory-scoped before any secondary filter is applied; preview responses whose `factory_id` differs from the requested/current factory are rejected by the frontend rather than displayed. Factory changes reset page-level filters so a value selected in one factory cannot hide or misrepresent another factory's statistics. Mac archive metadata (`._*`, `.DS_Store` and files under `__MACOSX`) is excluded before PO validation; a selection containing only metadata is rejected with a clear instruction to choose the same-name real file. Huaxing exposes BuzzBee, Dickie, Caixing, EDU, 360, Yinhui, SEASONS (Shixin), Maxx, Shushupapa, Barter and Disney; Huadeng exposes Casdon, Jakks, Simba, Spin and Goliath; the redundant Spin Master entry is removed; Huakang A exposes independently mapped 360, Green Toys and HeadStart profiles; Huakang D owns INDEX, JAZAWARES (legacy source spelling `JAZWARES`), MAXX, STROTTMAN, Disney, SEASONS (施信) and 优必选 (UBTECH). The Huakang D JP entry is removed. These two retired entries are absent from customer selection and rejected by active preview/export factory routing; their legacy parser specifications and historical records remain intact, including Huakang C JP compatibility. Shared customer codes such as `360` and `maxx` are dispatched by the explicit factory identifier, so same-name customers retain their factory-owned routing; Disney and SEASONS explicitly share PO parsing between Huaxing and Huakang D:

- Preview one or more customer purchase orders against the `.xlsx` Heyuan unified business schedule containing `ITEM表` (or explicitly mapped Huaxing ITEM classification sheets), `接单表` and `正单评审表` with the common row-3 fields (ITEM A:AB and summary A:S); customer-specific columns outside those areas are not a template-acceptance requirement.
- Return normalized fields, validation results and source lineage.
- Export a separately named complete copy of that unified schedule without mutating the source file.
- Require authenticated sales scope and the relevant customer-order read/export permissions.

For normal Customer Order Center operation, customer selection controls PO extraction and any explicitly registered customer extension; the base writer is `HEYUAN_BUSINESS_UNIFIED_SCHEDULE_V1`, with the Huakang A extension described below. All three sheets append within the active region above the first `取消单` marker. The writer finds the last actual order across the shared keys and uses the next trailing blank row, never backfills an earlier interior gap and never clears an existing populated/example row; when no trailing blank remains, it inserts immediately above `取消单` in all three sheets and copies the immediately preceding row's complete style, row height, validation and translated formula pattern. The `取消单` section and everything below it, including `已走货订单`, are user-owned and remain untouched except for the natural row shift caused by insertion. `ITEM表` writes only available mapped values in A:AB; this is an allowed-write range, not a required-field contract, so customer PO and reliable unique history values that do not exist remain blank, M is created only when both quantity and units-per-carton exist, and AC onward remains user-maintained. `接单表` and `正单评审表` receive only C:G copied exactly from `ITEM表` D:H; every other template formula/value is preserved. Customer-specific columns before the remarks area are writable only through an explicit per-customer rule, never by a generic guess. Only the order reference and product number are mandatory placement/deduplication keys; other absent common fields do not block export.

Huaxing BuzzBee, Dickie, Caixing, EDU, 360, Yinhui, Disney, Maxx, Shushupapa and Barter use `HEYUAN_BUSINESS_UNIFIED_HUAXING_V2` against the September 5/6 unified schedules in `Desktop/PO入排期/河源打包/河源最新排期/华兴排期`. Each detail and summary sheet appends independently above its own cancellation marker; new summary formulas reference the exact detail sheet and row. If a newly generated review HKD-price lookup carries the supplied template’s broken order-sheet anchor, it links to the exact paired new order-sheet price cell; existing rows and manually entered order prices remain untouched. BuzzBee uses four ITEM category sheets (子弹枪/水枪/套装/公仔); Dickie additionally uses `恐龙蛋口水车Iteam表`. Common headers alone determine acceptance. Product-only stock rows may provide product/category lookup but never order identity; real order history includes later sections. BuzzBee base items may acquire schedule variant suffixes only from unique same-order or same-customer evidence; unknown or ambiguous categories/variants remain blocked. EDU allocates its internal EDUHX reference from historical E per distinct customer contract/PO, not per uploaded workbook, with customer contract in D and PO in F. Multiple products of the same customer order may share the internal reference, but different customer orders cannot share a reference/product pair. EDU batch/history checks use the full customer contract/PO plus product rather than the newly allocated internal reference; complete composite contracts match completely, and legacy single-part contracts reconcile only with unambiguous history. Missing customer identity, ambiguous history and internal-reference collisions remain non-confirmable blockers, as do quantity conflicts. Multi-order EDU workbook revision selection is per customer order, retaining unrelated orders; conflicting same-version files require a verified revision rather than arbitrary last-file replacement. Manual changes recheck both customer business identity and internal reference. 360 E uses the full Release reference and may reconcile a unique historical child reference; existing multi-child Releases remain blocked for split review. Yinhui E/F are separate numeric SO/PO identities, without synthetic prefixes. Caixing E/F are confirmation/customer PO, Disney E/F are its PO, and Shushupapa F is the terminal customer PO. Packing composites use the right outer quantity. Legacy currency/country/label display strings do not enter national standards or packaging. Unique trailing remarks/contact/PDQ columns resolve by actual headers, while duplicate date-code extensions stay manual. Changed manual identities are rechecked for category and duplication; a previous duplicate confirmation cannot authorize a newly introduced duplicate. This batch did not supply a latest SEASONS schedule, so its existing mapping is unchanged.

Huakang A 360, Green Toys and HeadStart use `HEYUAN_BUSINESS_UNIFIED_HUAKANG_A_V4` for the approved September 4 unified workbook extensions. All customer schedules are accepted on their common public fields alone: ITEM A:AB and summary A:S. Headers outside these areas may be added, removed, renamed or reordered and never cause a whole-template rejection. Huakang A extensions resolve known fields by actual normalized headers; missing or ambiguous fields are skipped without writing into unrelated columns. Green Toys planned transport is recognized only between its unique port and USD-price headers, so its similarly named actual-shipping field remains manual. Price formulas use resolved columns, not fixed letters. The positions listed next describe the September reference files only, not required positions. Each sheet appends independently above its own first `取消单` marker because their boundary rows differ; any literal cell content prevents reuse. Reserved formulas remain authoritative, missing formulas are filled from a complete reserved summary row before falling back to history, and inserted rows preserve styles, row heights, validations, merges, print settings and ordinary/array formulas with adjusted references. ITEM D:H is copied to summary C:G at each sheet's own output row; new summary A/B/H:M/R:S formulas bind directly to that exact ITEM row so confirmed duplicates remain visible and broken reserved lookup formulas are not propagated. New summary remarks link only when the ITEM remarks header is unique. Existing order formulas remain untouched and summary prices remain manual, except for Green Toys orphan-summary recovery: when the current PO supplies the exact identity and both active summary sheets agree on C:G, with no corresponding ITEM history or conflicting/literal derived fields, export restores the missing ITEM detail and rebinds those existing summary-derived formulas while preserving the numbered identity and N:Q prices. Contradictory, one-sided or occupied identities fail clearly rather than being overwritten. New Green Toys exports validate each written three-sheet identity and direct formula pair before serialization. Long wrapped text expands only generated row heights. 360 maps Customer Release to D, the RL release to E/F, End Customer to G, Package Type to T, UPC to AD, container type to AE, Consolidated ID to AF and discharge port to AG. Green Toys maps country/port/transport to AD/AE/AG; HeadStart maps APN/explicit or unique historical printing requirement/country to AD/AE/AF. Both map USD price and USD/HKD amounts into AH:AK with 7.8 conversion, retaining the template multiplication precision. Inventory prices, production status, actual shipping and invoice columns remain user-owned. Cartons round quantity/pack upward. Legacy display strings containing prices, barcodes or transportation must never enter national-standard or packaging columns. Huakang A history reads real orders throughout ITEM, including later sections, excludes simulated rows using the actual remarks header, and falls back from empty SO to customer PO/Release identity. Green Toys deduplicates by base PO plus reconciled item, reuses the unique historical numbered PO (whose suffix does not follow OCR line order), and allocates new suffixes above existing ones. HeadStart normalizes Unicode item separators and its established accidental extra trailing A only for comparison. Unique history may supply missing product data, barcode, port or printing requirement; conflicting values remain blank. These rules remain factory-specific and retain duplicate-confirmation and manual-resolution controls.

Huadeng and Huakang D (except Disney and UBTECH) use `HEYUAN_BUSINESS_UNIFIED_REGIONAL_V3` with the same common-only header validation and independent per-sheet append/preservation rules as Huakang A. Latest reference directories are `Desktop/PO入排期/河源打包/华登排期` (September 5) and `华康D排期/华D排期`. History includes real orders in later sections and falls back from blank SO to contract/PO; simulated rows are excluded. Huadeng Caston is Casdon: D/E/F carry its own PO, the terminal customer's PO goes into the unique `客PO` extension, and country/container/brand extensions resolve by headers. Seasonal article suffixes may be reconciled only from a unique same-PO or same-customer-and-version historical match. Jakks E uses confirmation number; Simba E uses its already-combined release/line number and historical `12/48` packing means 48 per outer carton. Spin maps D to PO/line, E to the full material/Sales Order-line combination, F to customer PO, and H to material group. The legacy Spin Master parser shares this field identity but its redundant active entry has been removed; new imports use Spin only. Jakks non-piece units such as KGM are explicit quantity blockers and must not silently count as pieces. Jakks customer Confirmation CONTRACT PDFs are now available in 测试订单分类/JAKKS 正式PO; these contain PCS quantities and remain distinct from the older Chinese factory weight contracts. Native CONTRACT wrapped description suffixes and case counts are preserved. Explicit PO PRODUCT MEETS … STANDARD and native CONTRACT item NOTES packaging take precedence over schedule history; generic Excel remarks do not imply packaging; preserve the stated standard text without inventing a certification number. Huakang D 建文 uses STROTTMAN; JAZWARES/MAXX retain their existing PO engines. A missing MAXX packing value defaults to blank, never zero cartons. New summary formulas use the same exact ITEM-row linkage as Huakang A. D reference falls back to PO and detects quantity conflicts even where historical D/E are blank. Numeric cells misformatted as dates are read as Excel serial numbers only for quantity/packing; new quantity/packing/carton cells use numeric formats, while historical cells stay intact. Certificate presence flags and legacy display strings are not national standards or product packaging. Huakang D SEASONS (施信) reuses the existing Huaxing QF/formal PO parser, but only the uploaded Huakang D three-sheet unified schedule provides history and export targets. Its output uses HEYUAN_BUSINESS_UNIFIED_REGIONAL_V3; fingerprints, ledger identities and permissions remain factory-scoped. The Huaxing legacy schedule mapping is unchanged. Constructed inputs pass regression, but actual Huakang D PO/schedule business acceptance remains pending. Huakang D 优必选 (UBTECH) uses `HEYUAN_BUSINESS_UNIFIED_HUAKANG_D_UBTECH_V1` with the provided native-text RMB Purchase Order, unified workbook and mapping tutorial. It validates material rows, PCS quantities, per-line amounts and PO totals; E/F use the POORD identifier without the 量产 suffix, while contract/production numbers and historical carton marks/system status are not inherited. Incoming date is the selected customer-confirmation email date; shipment defaults to each PO requirement date and may be manually corrected without making that correction mandatory. Same-customer/item unique history may supply packing; cartons round upward. Public summary N:Q remains manual factory pricing and uses the workbook exchange-rate setting; historical factory prices never populate new rows. Seven explicitly named ITEM extensions contain source RMB unit price, order HKD unit/amount (RMB/0.85), order USD unit/amount (HKD/7.75), customer merchandiser UBTECH and merchandiser 林乐谊; missing extensions append after existing columns without shifting historical cells, and existing unique headers may move. Converted unit prices retain precision and amounts round to cents. Only new review rows link directly to their paired order price cells. The tutorial example amounts disagree with its formulas, so formulas are authoritative. Scanned/image-only PDFs and unsupported currencies/units fail clearly rather than guessing. Huakang D Disney explicitly reuses Huaxing Disney PO parsing and unified writing through an independent customer entry with `HEYUAN_BUSINESS_UNIFIED_HUAKANG_D_DISNEY_V1`. Its product/history lookup and output use only the uploaded current-factory workbook; factory permissions, preview fingerprints and export audits remain factory-scoped. Both factories expose one Disney entry. The factory total-schedule view displays only the current factory's batch; switching factories clears it and invalidates pending requests. This view does not yet persist imported orders across sessions. Uploaded common-template workbooks do not contain a reliable factory identity, so users must select the corresponding factory's schedule.

Goliath uses its September 5 unified schedule as the sole output template. The July 25 legacy schedule and the user-provided `Goliath客排货表 Ai.xlsx` (Ai sheet) are mapping references only. Its formal native-text PDF parser supports both Goliath Far East and Goliath BV European orders. It reads complete Order # into ITEM D/E, explicit regional customer PO into F (blank when absent), actual Deliver to into G, full item code into H and Qty into K. Far East explicit Cargo Ready/Ship Date maps to Z; European Requested delivery date from the product row maps to Z and explicit Casepack to L. Product-row delivery dates take precedence over stale document shipping notes, with both conflicting dates preserved in a visible review warning; the supplied PEU25_00642 revision has a user-confirmed product-table date of 2026-01-20 versus an obsolete 2025-01-20 note. English month parsing accepts SEPT as well as SEP and September. It extracts price by PDF column to preserve digits wrapped onto a second line, and requires a unique USD total that reconciles with the identified details; unknown product pages cannot be silently skipped. Missing product data/outer pack may use unique latest-schedule history, never legacy runtime data or item suffix guesses. Explicit inspection/label/netting notes map by meaning, with safety-netting notes in the unique remarks extension. Goliath alone populates new summary USD/HKD prices and amounts using USD × 7.75 per its Ai scheme; other profiles retain their existing price ownership. Date code is RR + three-digit day-of-year + final year digit, derived from shipment minus ten days and moved backward over weekends, with a visible holiday-review warning as explicitly authorized. The inspection-plus-three-day ship-date fallback also carries a holiday-review warning. Factory holidays are not configured.

Customer-order preview responses carry a deterministic `preview_fingerprint` over the selected factory/customer, received date, source file names and SHA-256 values, and mapping/output-template identity. Every export must submit that fingerprint; changed PO bytes, changed schedule bytes or a changed received date returns HTTP 409 and requires a fresh preview. Row-level hard blockers have a controlled manual-resolution path: the preview header opens a standalone resolution dialog (independent of the horizontally scrollable 17-field grid); when the issue identifies an editable field, the user may enter a replacement value that is written into the generated new-order workbook without modifying the source PO; otherwise the user may explicitly confirm the exceptional data and release the row. Every manual edit or confirmed release requires a 4–500 character reason. Test-stage duplicate confirmation remains separately controlled by the revocable `customer_order:duplicate_confirm` permission, while export-audit reads use `customer_order:audit_read`. Successful exports append immutable evidence in `customer_order_export_audits` (created by migration `20260804_0049` and extended for exact manual values by `20260807_0059`), including actor, source hashes, preview fingerprint, exact confirmed issue keys, manual field overrides/reason, output name/hash and timestamp; the controlled read endpoint is `GET /api/customer-orders/audits`. CPU-heavy workbook/PDF/OCR preview and export calls run in the Starlette worker pool so they do not block unrelated async API traffic. The versioned duplicate-order golden contract is `backend/tests/fixtures/customer_order_duplicate_contract_v1.json`.

The customer paragraphs below remain authoritative for PO recognition, revision selection, parsing, conversions and source-field lineage only. Their historical customer-specific schedule names, encryption behavior, insertion locations and output-column descriptions are superseded by the unified writer above for normal UI operation; compatibility branches remain only so saved historical previews and direct legacy API regression contracts can still be reconciled during migration.

BuzzBee accepts `.xls`/`.xlsx` PO workbooks. WMC is recognized by its dedicated homepage marker and keeps the WMC-specific mapping. WMU is a mainland Walmart US contract: its first sheet supplies the shared product number, English description, outer-carton pack, US profile and packaging reference, while every effective row in the required `PO Attached` sheet becomes a separate normalized order using that row's S/C number, Walmart PO, ordered quantity, ship window, declared cartons and inspection date. A declared-carton total that conflicts with ordered quantity divided by the shared outer pack is blocked rather than silently changed. Every other non-Indonesia ordinary contract is parsed by labels rather than being restricted to AAFES or by file extension. Ordinary customer/country metadata may span the contract-number row and the row above, while the safety standard is derived from explicit contract clauses or a known customer profile. The mapping updates its order/review/ITEM sheets and deliberately rejects only explicitly identified Indonesia contract or schedule variants. Dickie accepts scanned Simba Dickie Release Order PDFs, performs local OCR, splits a combined PDF at each `Release Order Page 1` into multiple order rows, handles ordinary, mixed-article and inferred dinosaur-product routing, and accepts legacy attachment rows with alphanumeric item suffixes, optional annotations, master-only contracts and OCR-spaced release references. Dickie ignores `990...` handling-charge rows and leaves multiply recognized or handwritten-revised prices blocked rather than guessing. Mixed orders write every child master contract with its allocated `pc` quantity, every available child PO and the mixed-product detail into the Dickie order/review/Iteam sheets; system-preparation quantities are deducted by attachment child item plus child contract instead of the parent mixed item, and same-parent batch order is preserved. Review cartons divide by the outer-carton quantity, the order sheet reads the requested ship-date column, and a calculated inspection date that lands on a weekend moves to the preceding workday. Customer Order Center keeps API parse failures and row-level blockers visibly listed on the import/preview page until the batch or selected files change. During the current test stage, a duplicate order/Reference detected in the uploaded schedule, history or same upload batch is presented as `待确认`; the user may explicitly confirm it and export the duplicate under the duplicate-confirmation policy. Missing business data, quantity conflicts and other row-level exceptions remain visibly blocked until the user supplies a supported manual value or explicitly confirms release with a reason. Both mappings preserve the uploaded schedule filename and return a workbook protected with the configured 2026 password.

Caixing accepts text-layer Playmates PDF POs with the observed OE/OL/OG/OH/OK prefixes and maps each order into the dedicated `正单评审表`、`接单表` and `ITEM表` schedule contract. A MIX group writes the product header above `MIX:` as the parent large-goods number and its assortment lines as detail goods; a PO without MIX uses the product itself for both roles. ASSORTMENT carton ratios are matched by the complete normalized child product code, including letter variants such as `40644AE24` and `40644EE24`, while the optional horizontal product matrix continues to use the numeric family column. US, EU and GULLIVER RUS/KZ remarks map to 美版彩盒、EU盒包装 and 俄罗斯彩盒包装 respectively. A missing optional horizontal matrix column is a visible warning that leaves that matrix cell blank but does not block export. Export preserves the source filename, legacy `.xls` encryption state and existing horizontal freeze split, while explicitly freezing the first two rows of `正单评审表`/`接单表` and the first three rows of `ITEM表`.

The eight additional Huaxing mappings reuse the established RR-PO customer engines behind the shared Customer Order Center preview/export contract. EDU accepts `.xls`/`.xlsx`/`.xlsm`, deduplicates PO revisions, assigns the next `EDUHX` number and derives inspection seven days before shipment with weekend rollback. 360 accepts contract and Release PDF/Excel inputs, generates rows only from Releases, uses matching contracts for price, keeps the newest RL revision and inherits current-schedule product, country and date-code references. Yinhui accepts native-text PDF, scanned PDF or Excel PO inputs; image-only PDFs use local OCR for reliable PO/SO, item, quantity, date and price fields, while untrusted Chinese OCR remarks remain blank for manual confirmation. It applies the fixed 7.75 USD-to-HKD conversion, derives inspection five days before shipment and validates line and amount-in-words arithmetic; an absent or printed `0/0` case pack is never guessed and instead becomes a row-level manual number field that is written into export after confirmation. SEASONS accepts QF or formal PO documents and only the SEASONS `正单评审表` schedule family; its fields are read from row 2, new orders reuse the reserved rows immediately below the last order in the first section (row 12 in the current schedule) and above the first total, and only the shortfall is inserted before that first total while style/formulas come from that section rather than later cancelled/shipped sections. Same-order/item duplicates can be explicitly confirmed for test export, while quantity conflicts remain blocked as modification orders. Maxx and Shushupapa accept PDF/Excel PO inputs and `.xlsx` schedules, strictly block cross-customer inputs, keep the newest PO revision and expose orders already present in current or shipped sheets as test-stage confirmation items; both derive inspection seven days before shipment, and Maxx also derives completion on the same date. Barter accepts scanned multi-page Purchase Contract PDFs and encrypted `.xlsx` schedules using password `2026`; it runs bounded local OCR per page, reconciles an inclusive TNT contract range from the filename with page order, maps Walmart PO, item, quantity, five-piece master pack, HKD price/amount, date code and delivery date, uses the selected customer-email date as incoming date, leaves BV inspection manual, and appends every confirmed row to both `Iteam表` and `接单表` before restoring the source password. Existing-contract history may correct OCR-empty or clearly implausible fields while incoming quantity remains contract-owned. Disney accepts the real DLR/WDW theme-park, TDSE, international F-order and Japan V-order PDF layouts, ignores TL/terms attachments, keeps the newest revision and derives inspection five days before shipment. Its exporter writes only `ITEM表`, `正单评审表` and `接单表`: existing ITEM groups that already use segmented subtotals keep that structure and receive the new detail before their subtotal; detail-only groups remain detail-only and retain their blank separator; a repeated contract/PO is inserted immediately below the last matching row. New product groups reuse reserved rows before creating their own subtotal. The linked review/order rows are inserted at the corresponding logical position, and every inserted detail or subtotal copies the nearest same-type source row's complete text style, border, fill, number format, row height and translated formula pattern. Disney date code and factory unit price are never inferred from PO data and remain row-level manual fields that may be supplied or explicitly confirmed blank. All eight exports copy the complete uploaded workbook and preserve every existing Sheet, historical row, format, formula, image/drawing and print setting. Huaxing 360 synchronizes every confirmed Release into the actual schedule's `Iteam表`, `正单评审表` and `接单表`: `Iteam表` reads row-3 fields, the other two sheets read row-4 fields, every sheet writes immediately below its last effective PO and above its total row, reserved blank rows are reused first, and only a blank-row shortfall is inserted before the total. The new Iteam row copies the last PO's style and formula pattern, while the review/order rows keep the template's original formulas linked to that Iteam row. Yinhui likewise synchronizes every confirmed row into `Iteam表`, `正单评审表` and `接单表` according to its own detected headers: it reuses the blank rows immediately below the last effective order and above the total row, inserts only the shortfall when no reserved row remains, copies the latest ordinary order row's complete cell styling—including font, fill, border, alignment, number format, protection and row height—without propagating a blue exception row or replacing template formats with generic system formats, uses the last complete order's formula pattern, and fills the Iteam trailing total/factory-price columns plus shipment date. EDU synchronizes the detected detail/Iteam sheet, `正单评审表` and `接单表`. SEASONS writes the first section of `正单评审表` and also routes each item into exactly one matching ITEM sheet plus its corresponding order sheet; an ambiguous or missing item lane stays blocked. Maxx/Shushupapa use their configured customer order sheets. Barter synchronizes `Iteam表` and `接单表`, preserving their calculated carton and amount formulas. Affected formulas/filter/merge/validation/print ranges are expanded, and the result is saved separately without overwriting the upload.

The five Huadeng mappings also reuse their established RR-PO engines behind the same normalized preview/export contract while remaining separate customer profiles. Casdon accepts PDF/Excel PO inputs, keeps the newest and most complete PO revision, converts USD at 7.75 and derives inspection seven days before shipment with weekend rollback. Each Casdon PO detail is inserted immediately after the last existing schedule detail with the same base item number and before that item's subtotal. Outer-carton pack is item-level master data across customers: the importer inherits it only when the uploaded schedule has one unique non-empty value for that base item; a missing or conflicting pack becomes a row-level manual number field that may be supplied or explicitly confirmed for test release. The total-carton cell is always a row formula dividing quantity by outer pack. Jakks accepts native standard CONTRACT PDFs, SUPPLEMENTARY CONTRACT PDFs (carton-mark information supplied before formal PO approval), WPS-converted Excel contracts and `.xls`/`.xlsx` schedules. Supplementary contracts may enter the unified schedule before the formal PO: rows are marked 补充合同 and preserve explicit contract/confirmation/customer-PO identifiers, SKU, quantity, outer pack, cartons, packaging and request date. Missing monetary values stay blank and do not inherit historical prices. Ambiguous quantity/price columns or contradictions with explicit case-count × outer-pack evidence are rejected. CXL filenames remain blocked; SUP filenames require supplementary-contract content verification. Exact/business-equivalent batch lines remain deduplicated, and later formal POs use the existing confirmation-number plus product history check to prevent unintended duplicate scheduling; no automatic replacement of existing rows is implied. Product/contact inheritance remains restricted to unique schedule values. Its native PDF parser accepts plain item numbers without hyphens, the implicit PCS quantity layout and the trailing-`USD` amount layout, and reads customer PO, confirmation/country, consignee, case count, outer pack, request date and notes from their labels. During export, PO-owned Jakks fields such as quantity, packing, prices and totals explicitly replace any copied historical formula while unmapped template formulas and the copied row format remain intact. Simba accepts PDF/Excel Release Orders, prefers a same-name WPS Excel over PDF, selects newer or more complete revisions and safely inherits exact-contract or unique-item schedule data. Image-only Simba PDFs automatically discover the deployed Tesseract executable through the shared OCR configuration, tolerate common OCR substitutions in `SC` references and zero-value packing fields, and expose per-file parser failures instead of replacing them with a generic empty-batch message. Simba export groups inserted details by ITEM and writes a separate copied-format total row with an ITEM-local quantity formula after each group instead of one combined total for the entire batch. Spin uses its `2026年未验货订单` schedule family, revision deduplication and customer-plus-item inheritance while leaving manual planning dates blank. Spin Master remains a distinct mapping using `SPIN排期`/`SPIN总汇`, `.xls`/`.xlsx` schedules and composite line deduplication. All five now export a complete copy of the uploaded customer schedule and insert confirmed rows only into that customer's mapped master Sheet; every other Sheet, historical row, formula, format, drawing and print setting is retained, and the uploaded file is never overwritten.

The Huakang A 360 mapping uses the legacy ThreeSixty `PURCHASE ORDER RELEASE` rules and requires a schedule workbook containing `360客排期表`. It accepts PO PDF or WPS-converted `.xls`/`.xlsx`/`.xlsm`, extracts the RL contract, revision and revision date, customer PO/release, item, quantity, carton quantity, planned inspection date, FCD, contact, container type, transportation mode and discharge port, and calculates total cartons by rounding quantity divided by carton quantity upward. Missing contract, item or quantity blocks export; a missing FCD remains a visible warning. Export copies the complete uploaded workbook and inserts confirmed orders after the final recorded PO. Each unique item first receives a copied product-title row matching the nearest preceding title pattern (such as rows 265/267): the item is written into merged A:C and the product name into merged D:H while font, size, bold state, border, alignment, row height and merge layout are preserved; its PO detail row or rows follow immediately below. Existing pending title rows and later content shift downward. PO `Revision Date` remains the entry-date value, all other Sheets, historical data and workbook presentation features are retained, and the result is saved separately without overwriting the upload.

The Huakang A Green Toys mapping accepts native `.html`/`.htm` POs and `.png`/`.jpg`/`.jpeg` image POs; its input identity is `HUAKANG_A_GREEN_TOYS_PO_V2`. Legacy schedule compatibility requires `2026年排期`; normal UI uses the unified three-sheet writer above. Native HTML is read only as a static Green Toys table, never executing scripts or requesting external resources, and requires unambiguous PO/date metadata, every detail, per-line arithmetic and a matching printed total. Image imports use local English Tesseract with pixel-detected seven-column ruled-table boundaries, separate header/cell/footer regions and physical-row TSV alignment, never screenshot-specific coordinates or filename-derived PO identities. A unique labeled PO and valid dates, semantic column headers, every physical detail, exact quantity/price/amount arithmetic and a unique matching printed Total are required before preview or save; missing or inconsistent evidence fails clearly instead of dropping lines or guessing numbers. Unsupported, cropped or unreadable layouts require a complete clearer image or native HTML. Source pixels are unchanged and no cloud recognition service receives customer files. Recognition is sequential and single-threaded, with a shared 45-second OCR deadline, per-call limits, an 8-megapixel input cap and bounded region upscaling; the existing outer disposable job budget remains authoritative. The lower-level legacy text parser retains suspicious item candidates for review and rejects missing/ambiguous labeled PO identities. Image parsing reads PO date/number, `Deliver By Date`, item, English description, quantity and USD price, reconciles an OCR item to a unique same-description item already in the uploaded schedule, and uniquely inherits Chinese product name, outer-carton quantity, country and discharge port by item. Customer inspection is one day before `Deliver By Date`, transport is fixed to `40'YT`, and USD converts to HKD at 7.8. OCR rows always remain visibly marked for review; missing carton quantity or other required facts block export. The HeadStart mapping accepts text-layer PDF POs and requires `2026年HS客排期表` (including the legacy trailing-space sheet name). It reads Order No, item/description, piece quantity, Delivery Date, APN, outer-carton quantity, carton count, date code and USD price; the schedule ship date is seven days after inspection and HKD uses 7.8. A legacy HeadStart contract with one accidental trailing `A` in the schedule is normalized only for duplicate comparison. Both exports preserve the complete workbook, group the incoming batch by item with copied merged title rows, preserve row formulas/styles, mark every inserted title/detail row with a pale-green fill, save separately, and treat same-order/item rows already in the uploaded schedule as explicit test-stage duplicate confirmations while quantity differences remain blocked.

The five Huakang C mappings reuse the legacy multi-customer engine with strict customer detection, batch revision deduplication and unique item-to-product-name inheritance from the uploaded schedule. All accept PO PDF or WPS-converted `.xls`/`.xlsx`/`.xlsm` and `.xls`/`.xlsx`/`.xlsm` schedule templates. The selected import date acts as the legacy email-confirmation date and overrides PO Date; USD prices use the fixed 7.75 HKD conversion. INDEX maps PO/date, Ex-Factory, product, quantity, carton packing and USD price. JAZAWARES uses the legacy `JAZWARES` document markers and maps PO revision, contract, product, PCS/CTN, shipment and price. Its export follows the schedule's existing rows 18–30 group pattern: all small-PO detail rows for one parent item are appended together, followed by exactly one `parent item 合计` row; known size suffixes such as `-S`/`-M`/`-L` are removed only for the parent grouping key. New details inherit the nearest ordinary active detail row rather than a cancelled or highlighted manual-adjustment row, and totals inherit the latest item-total row, preserving all 53 columns' borders, fonts, sizes, fills, number formats, formulas and row heights. Huakang C MAXX maps P.O./S.C., product, quantity, delivery and price while leaving absent carton fields blank, independently from Huaxing MAXX. STROTTMAN converts Case quantities from Special Instructions into PCS and pieces-per-carton, writes the earliest split shipment to the main date column and preserves all shipment dates in remarks. JP maps the Huakang car-cover Chinese purchase order into the JP internal schedule and writes only explicitly provided factory prices; because the legacy system has no real JP PO and manually confirmed output sample, every JP result remains visibly marked for field-by-field review. All five export a complete copy of the uploaded workbook and append confirmed rows to the customer Profile's existing target Sheet, preserving the remaining Sheets, historical orders, formulas, formatting, drawings and print settings without overwriting the upload.

Huakang C MAXX additionally extracts the `Project#` project number/name and `TERMS OF DELIVERY`. Export follows the target Sheet's row-1 field meanings and rows 2–8 detail-plus-total pattern: small items are grouped under the project big-item number, trailing total placeholders are reused or cleared, existing broken totals are repaired, exactly one total row follows each new project, and absent carton fields remain blank. MAXX detail and total rows inherit their respective complete styles, formulas and row heights.

Huakang C STROTTMAN uses the actual `建文客排期表` target Sheet with row 3 as the field header and rows 4–10 as the layout reference. Each product is appended as a copied merged item/name title row followed by its detail rows. Detail mapping writes material/production readiness, item and product fields, the email-confirmation entry date, numeric PO contract suffix, PCS quantity, earliest FCD, customer/country/contact, pieces per outer carton and USD price; total cartons and HKD/USD amounts remain row-relative formulas, factory price remains manual, and its total stays blank until a price is entered. Case quantity is converted from Special Instructions to PCS, while all listed shipment dates remain in remarks. Whitespace-only template placeholders do not move the insertion boundary.

For the unified complete-copy export, inserted date fields are stored as real Excel date values so retained row formulas continue to calculate. The active UI accepts only `.xlsx` schedules; legacy `.xls`/`.xlsm` customer-schedule conversion and preservation rules apply only to the migration-compatibility API branches described above.

There is not yet a persistent normalized customer-order ledger, immutable order-version model, confirmed-demand publication contract or downstream PMC integration. The export audit table is evidence for generated artifacts and manual confirmations only; it is not an authoritative order ledger. Frontend ledger, scheduling, exception and feedback examples are not authoritative production data.

### Carton Procurement Collaboration

The carton release schema follows main's immutable module-feedback merge `20261005_0131`: schema-only fabric-source prerequisite `20261005_0134`, carton assets `20261005_0135`, and private carton feedback `20261005_0136`. The local development SQLite records published head `20261008_0146` and passes the current application schema guards. Earlier unpublished `0131`–`0133` IDs are historical; applied migration files remain untouched. Any future reconciliation requires an offline API, a verified full backup and comparison of the affected schema/data contracts; never blindly stamp it or replay table creation. The schema prerequisite does not enable fabric routes, pages or permissions in this release.

Carton file computation uses disposable processes with a 240-second deadline, Linux 1 GiB address-space/CPU limits, one concurrent carton file worker per API container, bounded uploads and private temporary-file cleanup. Weekly, inspection and delivery imports and combined order exports use owner- and factory-scoped polling tasks; only the separate completion request can create the audited import batch, under current authorization and the existing factory transaction lock. Retrying completion returns the same batch; completed tasks do not prevent a new upload after withdrawal. At most eight unexpired task results are retained for 30 minutes, reclaiming completed/failed slots. The task registry is local to one API process and is lost on restart; use one API worker until a shared task store is implemented. A missing task requires checking durable import history before retrying. Legacy import endpoints, historical order/inventory imports and master imports also isolate workbook decoding, but retain their existing synchronous confirmation contracts. Business writes, preview fingerprints, permissions and immutable source evidence remain authoritative in the API transaction. Generic weekly/delivery parsing no longer silently truncates at 500 rows: accept up to 5000 parsed rows, otherwise reject the entire input. ITEM sheet physical-row limits and the stricter 500-row supplier delivery template remain enforced; sheets over 256 columns are rejected. Matching builds request-local identity indexes while preserving customer, SO/PO, ambiguity and split rules. These changes are deployed without a schema migration. Production read-only PostgreSQL checks cover factory paging/statistics, search execution and batch-projection equivalence to legacy order output; local large synthetic/SQLite regressions and the isolated Linux parser smoke test do not establish production large-volume throughput or authenticated real-file business acceptance.

Top-level navigation has eight areas in this order: 工作看板, 订单管理, 收料入库, 库存管理, 异常处理, 月结对账, 基础资料, 操作日志. 订单管理 contains 订单台账, 排期核对与交期提醒 and 箱唛资料库; 库存管理 contains 实时库存, 收发汇总, 库存流水 and 库存盘点. Existing weekly-check and inventory-summary links remain valid and highlight their parent area; inventory-movements opens a separate ledger page. Real-time inventory no longer stacks the full transaction ledger underneath. 收料入库 switches between 待收订单, 供应商发货待收 and 收料历史, defaulting to pending orders. The supplier receiving queue embeds the original receipt, difference, bin-allocation and reversal-correction workflow, with a separate 已核实记录 view retaining no-order sample associations and a 冲销 entry for posted receipts. That entry fetches the fresh original receipt and opens the existing reason/confirmation/revision workflow; factory/account/authorization changes and unmount invalidate pending reads. Receiving and supplier-feedback lists combine customer, status, association, keyword and selected delivery/acceptance-date filters with stable date sorting. Legacy factory/shipment links redirect to this integrated page; the internal delivery-import upload and empty-review UI are retired. The 工作看板 starts with a prominent pending-work panel combining actionable business weekly-schedule alerts and the current factory's supplier delivery notes awaiting warehouse confirmation. Supplier cards read the current factory's authorized Dongkang receiving queue directly through `/api/carton-supplier/internal/shipments/pending`, requiring procurement read, receipt_write and inventory_write. SENT notes and notes linked to reversed receipts appear, including administrators' factory work even when their personal work-center todo is empty; posted receipts and resolved non-arrivals leave the queue. Count and bounded preview rows share one SQL snapshot. Cards link to the exact receipt/correction, show query failure instead of a false empty queue, discard prior-factory/context responses, and refresh on dashboard entry/manual refresh and work-center bell updates. Personal notification read/snooze/archive and responsibility assignment do not filter this factory queue; the receiving workflow owns completion. The page header provides a read-only 使用教程 with searchable chapters and explicitly marked demonstration illustrations, covering master setup, opening stock, optional historical orders and the daily dashboard → schedule → purchase → supplier shipment → warehouse receipt → outbound workflow, plus corrections, month-end reconciliation and concrete failure handling. It can print the complete guide after clearing search, and its workspace shortcuts preserve the current factory and receiving permissions without creating business records. Tutorial text and the standalone illustrated manual share the same content source. The supplier collaboration header also offers a lazy-loaded supplier-specific guide covering issued purchases, acceptance/due commitments, document/ERP exports, original delivery imports, effective warehouse feedback, current-version monthly confirmation, contract-shared carton-mark original files and failure recovery. Both guides use the same searchable/printable reader with separate audience content; supplier shortcuts open only existing supplier workspaces or carton marks and do not submit business actions. Opening/closing preserves filters and drafts. Both standalone manuals are synchronized from their respective chapter sources by `scripts/sync-carton-usage-guides.mjs`. Internal month-end instructions distinguish the supplier-first confirmation sequence, independent inventory closing and cutover balances/old unsettled accounts. Only the selected workspace is displayed; navigation preserves entry drafts and filters, and history refresh does not change the selected page. Navigation changes do not merge business records, exception handling states or month-end snapshots.

The dashboard's four statistic cards navigate to orders, weekly checks, inventory and open exceptions. Both internal carton procurement and Dongkang supplier collaboration headers offer one combined 反馈与变更 entry, retaining separate compose, own-feedback and feature-update tabs; administrator processing remains internal. `/api/carton-feedback` persists factory-scoped employee feedback, private screenshots, immutable administrator replies and published update notes in published migration `20261005_0136` (local unpublished `20261005_0133`); existing stores require an explicit backup/migration before startup, and downgrade refuses to discard populated tables. Internal feedback requires current-factory canonical procurement read; the supplier portal instead reuses canonical carton_supplier:read and active Dongkang service-factory/issued-order scope, granting no internal procurement access. Supplier reads and screenshot downloads additionally require that exact author and /carton-supplier context, including when an administrator enters through the supplier portal. Suppliers choose the relevant authorized service factory in the dialog. Employees and suppliers see only their own feedback, while the separate canonical `system:feedback_manage` permission permits factory-scoped internal administrator processing and publication for both sources. Business management positions do not inherit that system permission. Replies require text and a revision check, with 待处理 / 已修改 / 暂时无法修改 states; employees read the response in 我的反馈. Feature changes are administrator-published notes targeted to INTERNAL employees or SUPPLIER Dongkang users in the current factory. Published audience migration 20261006_0137 (local unpublished 20261006_0134) preserves every old note as INTERNAL and requires backup/migration before the new API starts; downgrade refuses to erase an existing SUPPLIER audience. Supplier workspace reads force SUPPLIER filtering, ordinary internal readers get INTERNAL only, and administrator publication/list selection explicitly chooses the audience. Publication idempotency includes the audience. These are not employee feature requests or cross-factory broadcasts. Feedback and update publication retries use actor/factory-scoped idempotency keys. Each feedback accepts up to three PNG/JPG/WebP screenshots of at most 5 MiB and 16 million pixels each; the server re-encodes them as PNG without source metadata and returns them only through authenticated owner/administrator access with private no-store responses. Image order is persisted so numbered notes keep their intended screenshot. Browser capture requires user-selected tab sharing, stops all tracks after capture/cancellation and falls back to paste/file selection; local canvas annotations support up to five numbered regions per screenshot. Closing or changing account/factory discards unsent drafts and private image URLs, and superseded reads cannot repopulate old content. Failed permission refreshes clear cached details and screenshots. Feedback and published-update lists apply authorized-factory/owner scope, literal keyword, submission/publication date and feedback-state filters before count and pagination. The UI shows 25 records per page with separate feedback/update offsets and selectable ascending/descending order; reply history remains limited to the newest 100 entries. Querying older feedback never broadens employee access to another author. Tutorials and the standalone manual cover feedback and updates. This workflow does not replace operational exceptions, alter orders/inventory, or add external notifications.

Carton procurement and nearby supplier, master-data and warehouse actions must show the specific failure reason during the current workflow, including when a modal is open. Procurement and supplier operations use a dismissible persistent notice above their dialogs; existing field and dialog errors remain visible. Shared HTTP feedback prioritizes the server business reason, identifies validation fields and detail-row positions, and supplies Chinese network, timeout, status and unknown-error fallbacks when no business reason exists. Timeout feedback explicitly leaves the write outcome unconfirmed and requires checking the ledger or history before retrying. Persisted orders, receipts, inventory movements, supplier acceptance/shipments and master imports retain their success state when only a later refresh or document download fails; the notice states the completed stage and the remaining failure with its cause, preventing duplicate submissions. Zero-row schedule/receipt imports retain their batch evidence but visibly report parser warnings instead of presenting an empty import as business success. Factory-scoped late responses and errors are discarded after a factory switch in these affected action paths.

The carton `基础资料` workspace is implemented for entry convenience and mistake prevention (migration `20260908_0104`, with a startup schema guard). UI terminology uses 产品名称 for the product, 纸品类型 for packaging type and 纸质 for paper quality. Date labels distinguish 下单日期, 送货日期, 记账时间 and 最近入库日期 according to their actual data source. Its UI has two pages: 基础设置 (default) and 货号与包装. 基础设置 shows one factory-common date card, real customers with effective date/identifier rules and expandable per-customer CONTRACT associations, WORKSHOP and warehouse-grouped stable bin lists. The separate warehouse ACCESS authorization UI and write API are retired; old records remain for compatibility but never grant access or reserve warehouse names. CONFIG paper sets retain their own searchable page with active/inactive and preferred-only filters plus clear-all. CONFIG paper lines and order paper lines accept optional per-carton net_weight_kg/gross_weight_kg (kilograms, nonnegative, at most four decimals; gross must be at least net on each paper). Net is the goods weight per packed carton excluding the carton, cards and other packaging; gross is the complete packed carton including those materials, rather than the empty paper's weight. Both are optional reference values for the particular packaging configuration; saved order values remain independent snapshots. The order form, configuration editor, configuration list and historical sources show weights beside the corresponding paper, rather than as editable whole-order fields. Selecting a CONFIG, copying an order or reusing a history suggestion carries each paper's independent weights. Creation may default omitted weights only from an unambiguous matching paper in the revision-validated CONFIG; explicit null remains unknown. Order edits preserve omitted weights for the existing paper slot, including repeated material keys, validate the merged pair and treat weight changes as structural edits under existing locks. Saved orders, supplier issue line snapshots, audit evidence and formal configuration provenance retain the line weights independently of later master changes. Known paper weights participate in configuration identity; unknown weights retain old signatures. The configuration Excel template appends optional 每箱净重kg/每箱毛重kg columns, validates each paper and retains compatibility with the original template without those columns. The historical-order main template inserts 外箱每箱净重kg and 外箱每箱毛重kg at I/J immediately after H; this pair belongs only to the outer-carton demand, never to inner/carton-card demands. Historical previews show both weights, supplemental and legacy vertical rows accept their own pair, and missing columns or blank cells remain unknown while explicit zero is preserved. Invalid/duplicate weight columns, more than four decimals, gross below net or main-sheet weights without corresponding demand block import rather than losing or guessing weight data. Legacy root CONFIG and order-header weight values remain compatible historical evidence and are shown separately when known; they never default or get distributed into paper rows, and do not distinguish current configuration content. Existing immutable identities and original source snapshots remain unchanged; current canonical content and canonical source aliases reuse the original CONFIG instead of enrolling a duplicate solely because legacy header weights are absent. Local migration 20261008_0140 added nullable header columns and the responsibility/source-link tables on unpublished parent 20261007_0138; additive successor 20261008_0141 adds nullable paper columns without backfilling or changing existing business values. Startup rejects missing columns and downgrades refuse evidence deletion. Published forward revisions 20261008_0144 and 20261008_0145 carry these customer/weight additions after the published fabric 0143 head. The already-applied local alias lineage remains independent and backed up; do not replay local fabric migrations or rewrite applied historical migrations. Contract/item searches expand matching contracts within their customer and narrow the contract list to those matches. Contract associations default to active records and support active/inactive/all filtering. Their Delete action explicitly confirms revision-checked, audited deactivation through the existing master-save contract; it preserves customer identity, item associations, original orders, stock/accounting evidence and immutable provenance. Inactive contracts cannot authorize new order creation or confirmation and are not revived by history enrichment. Authorized master maintainers may restore that same record from the inactive list. Existing customer records are reused directly in the settings list; 新增客户/资料 opens a compact customer editor, and 规则 opens that customer's scoped rule editor. There is no separate customer-manager list or factory-default pseudo-customer row. Rule entry reuses existing IDs/revisions including inactive records and locks the customer/factory scope, so it cannot accidentally create duplicate defaults. Customer and place maintenance expose deactivation rather than deleting historical references. Warehouse controls are hierarchical: 添加仓库 creates a warehouse with its first required bin using existing location records; each warehouse exposes scoped 添加仓位 and master-only whole-warehouse rename and unused-warehouse deletion actions. Deletion requires explicit UI confirmation and server-side factory locking, the complete bin ID/revision set, and no inventory, receipt (including draft/voided), stocktake or business-history reference. Net-zero stock does not erase usage; earlier names remain checked through immutable audit evidence. Only never-used warehouse/bin records can be deleted, with a complete audit snapshot and removal of same-factory name-based maintenance grants in the same transaction; used warehouses remain available for historical lookup and may be deactivated through their bins. System unknown locations cannot be deleted. Bin creation from a warehouse locks that warehouse in the form. Warehouse rename atomically checks the full bin ID/revision set, keeps bin IDs and quantities, updates same-factory name-based maintenance grants with revision/audit evidence, and rejects occupied target names to prevent merging warehouses. Obsolete ACCESS records cannot block a rename. The stable system unknown-location ID is not editable or renamable; it is resolved through transfers. The order-page customer maintenance shortcut remains removed. New blank orders start with no selected customer. The searchable picker preserves typed customer names and codes while editing, clearing any previously selected identity; leaving the picker or pressing Enter automatically binds only a unique exact normalized active match. Enter during Chinese composition remains owned by the input method. Partial or ambiguous matches require explicit selection, duplicate names show distinct customer codes, and a manually selected customer remains selected when leaving the field. This interaction is locally verified and awaits frontend release. Only master-manage users may open the existing customer editor from a typed new name; creation requires explicit review/save and then selects the saved customer while preserving the open order. Matching inactive customer names cannot be recreated through this picker. Existing order editing preserves its customer. Shared master configuration selection never assigns or replaces the order customer; a new order opened from a configuration still requires customer selection. Customer creation remains separate from automatic historical configuration enrichment. Switching factories clears old ledger/customer state, closes customer editing, starts a new factory load despite an outstanding old load, and discards stale responses using factory plus generation. Customer saves validate the original form factory and existing ID and cannot degrade an edit into a create; old save results cannot affect a new edit session. Complete formal historical orders (including cancelled orders with formal-confirmation evidence) and accepted historical imports automatically populate configurations and associations; ordinary drafts and OCR previews do not. Later formal confirmation enriches them incrementally. Order-entry contract suggestions remain scoped to the selected customer; item/product suggestions use active CONFIG records shared across customers within the current factory; matching ignores case, whitespace and common identifier separators. Contract selection only fills its number; item/product selection explicitly copies one complete paper configuration including units and packing counts without defaulting price, quantity or dates. New order and added paper rows start with blank paper type, quality and specification, ignoring remembered values for these three fields. Each supports manual entry, an explicit dropdown, and normalized partial historical matches while typing; selection alone copies a value. The full configuration assistance card stays hidden until item/product matches or a relevant validation reminder exists. 基础设置 includes a paper-options card; privileged users maintain paper_types, paper_qualities and specifications as bounded, deduplicated arrays in the existing factory-wide RULE JSON, preserving its other data/revision/audit. A dedicated paper-options editor preloads all effective values and supports adding, replacing and removing them; hidden_paper_types, hidden_paper_qualities and hidden_specifications prevent removed historical values from returning as suggestions. These option settings remain effective independently of the shared date RULE status, and paper editing preserves unrelated RULE fields. Candidate lists combine factory options, active CONFIG values and factory-scoped paper_history from saved non-cancelled orders, including pending orders; saving an order refreshes suggestions.
